- **The interface** is the IndexedDB operations that `app` takes: open with creation or without, delete a database with the answer `"deleted"`, `"blocked"` or `"failed"`, read and write transactions with `get`, `entries` by keys or by an index, `count`, `put` and `delete`. The tests in Node run the real rules of erasure, and only the engine is replaced — an in-memory store with the key order of IndexedDB, cloning of records and the rollback of a failed transaction. Each connection closes on `versionchange`; opening without creation does not create a database — in Chromium an aborted upgrade of a new database does not leave it behind; `blocked` is an answer at once, without waiting, and IndexedDB does not cancel the deletion itself: it will go through when the other connection closes.
- **Firebase Auth** keeps the database `firebaseLocalStorageDb` and does not close its connection on `versionchange` — only on `pagehide` (section 4). So erasing this database on another sign-in project, while another tab holds the former one, is `"blocked"`: the database is not erased yet — the deletion will go through when that tab closes, — and `app` counts the erasure as not done: it does not clear the web storage, the registry keeps the former project, the next start checks again, and the Firebase sign-in in this tab is not set up. On sign-out `app` does not erase the database of Firebase Auth: `signOut()` removes the session record (section 3.2).
- **The storage's start** is after `/deployment.json` is read and before the app, one per tab: a repeat of the erasures from the registry, the check of the sign-in project — and while a sign-out waits for an erasure, — the work of correct submissions whose words were not waited for, and the time limit — attempts and lessons that did not change for 30 days, and the cache that was not accessed for 30 days, for each database separately. A step that failed holds neither the others nor the app. No registry — the start creates nothing; the registry could not be read — there is no check, and the start says just that, `"failed"`.
- **A connection taken away without `app`.** The browser closed a connection — `app` opens the registry again, without creation: no registry that the tab saw — the screen "The data on this device was erased"; the registry is there — "The data on this device can't be read", and nothing is erased. A `versionchange` came — `app` opens the same database again, without creation: another tab opened it at a new version — an offer to reload; erased it — a sign-out, a deleted profile — nothing. Both screens have "Reload", and after the screen the tab sends nothing more to the storage. Its own registry, erased by this same tab, is no sign that the browser erased the data.

— *Why:* the plan's proposals of T18, approved by the author. An interface at the level of operations, not of erasure events, keeps the rules of section 3.2 in one code, which both the tests in Node and the tests in Chromium check. The registry before the opening and the mark before the deletion keep a database from staying without a row, and a tab from creating anew an empty database that another erased. A lesson's key without the digest holds no more than one state per lesson, as section 3.4 tells.

— *Rejected:*
- `fake-indexeddb` in Node: a new `dev` dependency, and section 3.2 already tells to substitute an in-memory store, and Chromium checks the real IndexedDB;
- a fake at the level of erasure events: the tests would check the fake, not the rules;
- random names for profile databases: the registry would link them to the profile ID anyway, and a name by the ID is checked against the profile list of `api` without an extra table;
- the writing history as one record per attempt: each stroke would rewrite it whole, up to a few MB;
- erasing `firebaseLocalStorageDb` on sign-out: the database is held by the SDK's own connection, and `signOut()` removes the session anyway;
- waiting for `deleteDatabase` after `blocked`: the start would wait until the other tab is closed.

— *Consequence:* T19 takes the device's language from `novatrail-settings`, T21 the cache, T22 the registry, the check of the sign-in project and the erasures of sign-out, another account, "no access", the profile list and a deleted account, T24–T27 the profile database. That IndexedDB behaves so in WebKit and Firefox too is Д13 in table 4 of RUN.md. — [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.2, 3.7, 3.8 and 4; RUN.md, T18.

— *Amendment 2026-11-10:* a record of the store `views` of the shared cache is not bare bytes but the view as `api` gave it: `{ json?, version?, skillTiers?, usedAt }` — the JSON bytes, the format version, and for the skill graph the tiers too, — and a part that the module does not have lies as a record without `json`, so as not to ask about it again. A view of a format version outside the build's window does not go into the cache. The stores and indexes are the same, and the schema version stays 1: only the record changes. So it was done at T21. — [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.2, "Code".

— *Amendment 2026-11-12:* the profile database keeps the pictures of the work — the PNG of the sheet's crop and the crops of the workbook's boxes — not as a `Blob` but as bytes: `{ bytes, type }`, an `ArrayBuffer` and the type beside it, in the records of `submissions` and `correctWork`. `app` reads the bytes from the canvas's `Blob` before the transaction, and on reading gives a `Blob` again. So the author decided, the answer to В51, option (a): WebKit in an ephemeral session does not write a `Blob` into IndexedDB — `put` fails with `UnknownError: Error preparing Blob/File data to be stored in object store`, — and the work was not saved (T34; section 4). The stores, keys and indexes are the same, and the schema version stays 1: only the record changes, as with the 2026-11-10 amendment above. The `Blob` records that schema version 1 already holds, `app` does not migrate but reads as they are: a picture is either bytes or a `Blob`, and a record of another kind — work that the build does not read — gives "no work", stays in place and goes by the common rules: with the attempt's end, the time limit or an erasure. An attempt's submissions are read by a transaction of their own, after the attempt and the history: a record that the browser will not give back does not take the attempt and the writing history with it — without this the task screen would start the attempt anew and erase the history. In the same way the work of the correct submission is read before the transaction that erases the attempt, not in it: otherwise such a record would break the erasure too. The executor decided it in the planning of T38: the author was unavailable, and by the author's one-time instruction of 2026-11-12 tasks T36–T48 went one after another without the author; the author looks at the decision (RUN.md, T38, «Решено при планировании»). Rejected: a migration to bytes on opening the database — it requires version 2, and an upgrade transaction does not live through a read of a `Blob`, which is not an IndexedDB request: the migration would go as a separate pass at the start, and a record whose `Blob` cannot be read it would erase or leave — the same reading of two kinds, but with a new version as well, and the tabs of the former build would get an offer to reload. The records of version 1 live no longer than the attempt or 30 days, and in an ephemeral session of WebKit there were none. — [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.2, "How the work is written" and "Code"; RUN.md, T38.

## Dictionaries, the theme and the accessibility checks

**R17. The strings of the screens of `app` come from the TypeScript dictionaries `en` and `ru`, which `tsc` checks; the device's language is the adult's choice in the device's settings, and the language buttons stand on the home screen until the sign-in screen; the theme is light, blue; in CI each screen passes `axe-core` by WCAG 2.2 A and AA, targets of 44 × 44 CSS px and layout at 320 CSS px, in three languages and in two browser modes.** The author's answer of 2026-11-10 in the planning of T19 — the theme's colors, by the recommendation; the rest are the plan's proposals, which the author approved together with it (RUN.md, T19, «Решено автором»). The design is in [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.1, 3.8 and 3.9, "Code", and the sources in section 4.
- **The dictionaries** are `src/i18n/en.ts`, the source, and `src/i18n/ru.ts`, `as const satisfies Translation<typeof en, "ru">`: the same keys and not one other, a string where there is a string, and the plural forms of its own language — `one`, `few`, `many` and `other` — where there are forms. The placeholders are checked by the type `SamePlaces` by the string literals, and a Russian string with another placeholder name is an error of `tsc`. The words of the failure screens moved there from `src/boot/words.ts`, and the string for whoever deployed a copy stayed in English outside the dictionaries (the 2026-11-02 amendment to R09). A code from `api` that the build does not know gets the common string `common.unknown`.
- **The device's language.** `Boot` reads the choice from the device's settings (R16) together with the request of `/deployment.json` and draws the first screen when it has read it, but waits no longer than a second: a hung opening of IndexedDB will not leave the page empty. Not read — `navigator.languages`; a tag that the build does not know — lookup by truncation. In a frame the settings are not read. The choice is buttons in a `fieldset` with the legend "Language", each one the name of a language in that language itself, "English" and «Русский», and with its own `lang`; the chosen one is `aria-pressed`. A tap changes the screen's language at once, writes it into the settings and, if it was written, tells the other tabs through the tab's one persistent `BroadcastChannel` `novatrail-settings` — a channel does not hear its own messages. The chosen button differs not only by color — by an underline and bold type: forced colors take its colors away. Until the sign-in screen (T22) the buttons stand on the home screen — it is the screen before sign-in, — and T22 moves them, and E3 puts them into the section for adults as well.
- **The theme** is `src/theme/theme.css`, light, the primary color `#1d4ed8`, and the token table of section 3.9: text `#1a1c22`, secondary `#4b5263`, border `#6b7280`, "Correct" `#15803d`, "Not yet" `#b45309` — amber, not red, — error `#b91c1c`, marks `#2563eb`, as the canvas has. A test in Node reads the tokens from `theme.css` itself and checks each pair: text 4.5:1, borders, focus and marks 3:1. The canvas gets the same colors by its properties `--novatrail-canvas-*`. The font is the system one.
- **The screen** is `src/app/Screen.tsx`: `<main lang>`, the heading `h1` and the document title in the screen's language — "{screen} — NovaTrail", and for a screen whose heading is "NovaTrail" itself, just "NovaTrail", — and the focus on `h1` when the screen replaced another. Which screen is the first is decided by the screen instance's own token, not by a counter: under StrictMode a screen mounted twice is the same one.
- **The checks in CI**: `axe-core` 4.14.0 — in `devDependencies`, by the exception MPL-2.0 `dev` in `licenses.json` — by the rules with the tags of WCAG 2.0, 2.1 and 2.2 of levels A and AA, outside the subtree of the canvas element; targets — no smaller than 44 × 44 CSS px; at a width of 320 CSS px — no sideways scrolling and no content wider than its element; in `en`, `ru` and a pseudo-language. The Vitest projects `forced colors` and `reduced motion` — files of their own and a mode of the browser context from the very start, `contextOptions` of the Playwright provider, as in `canvas`.

— *Why:* the author's answer is blue: the same hue as the canvas's marks, only darker, and the canvas and the app look like one whole. The rest are the plan's proposals. A dictionary type by string literals catches a missing translation and placeholder at `ci-lint`, not on the learner's screen. The language buttons are needed before the sign-in screen, and there is no other screen before sign-in yet. The check only by WCAG 2.2 A and AA is the standard of R11: the best-practice rules would fail the test on what the standard does not require.

— *Rejected:*
- a translation library — i18next, FormatJS: R10 already rejected it, and the own code on `Intl` is a few functions;
- colors in TypeScript with generated CSS — a second source of colors; the test reads `theme.css` itself;
- a `<select>` list of languages — WCAG 3.2.2, section 3.8;
- `axe-core` with all rules, best-practice too, — the argument above;
- the names of languages from `Intl.DisplayNames` — it writes «русский» in lower case;
- teal `#0f766e` and violet `#6d28d9` as the primary — the plan's options, which the author did not choose.

— *Consequence:* Д11 closed, the CI part of Д10 too, and the manual checks of Д10 stay for E4. T20 takes `keyFor` for the words of refusals and outcomes, T22 — the language buttons onto the sign-in screen and `languageCode` of Firebase Auth by the screen's language, T23 and T24 — the check of `lang` of the module's text blocks through `:lang()`, T24 — the blink of "Check" under `reduced motion`. — [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.1, 3.7, 3.8, 3.9 and 4; RUN.md, T19.

— *Amendment 2026-11-10:* the dictionaries `src/i18n/en.ts` and `src/i18n/ru.ts` are not part of SonarCloud's duplication search — `sonar.cpd.exclusions` in `.sonarcloud.properties`. The Russian dictionary repeats the keys of the English one by design, and `tsc` checks them, above; since T22 their strings gave 3.0 % duplication in new code and failed SonarCloud's threshold of 3 %. An edit of the code would not remove the duplication: R17 did not choose another form of dictionary in which the keys do not repeat. SonarCloud still searches for other duplications (RUN.md, T22).

## The contract client

**R18. `app` calls `api` by its own thin layer: connect-es clients of the seven services of `novatrail.api.v1` behind three interceptors of the contract's rules ([the contracts text](../spec/contracts/README.md), section 15), a failure of a call by one type, and `app` writes words only for those outcomes and refusals that any screen shows the same way.** The author's answers of 2026-11-10 in the planning of T20 — the layer and the words, both by the recommendation; the rest are the plan's proposals, which the author approved together with it (RUN.md, T20, «Решено автором»). The design is in [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.7, "Code", and the sources in section 4.
- **The transport** is `createConnectTransport` of connect-web, Connect and binary protobuf ([the contracts text](../spec/contracts/README.md), section 9), the call's deadline is 30 s, the limit of `api` itself. A refusal of `fetch` — a network failure — becomes `unavailable`. The open views by Connect GET without a deadline are T21.
- **The interceptors**, from the outside in:
  - the device's actions: `CAUSE_ACCOUNT_GONE` — sign-out and then the erasure of the whole account (R16), once even with two refusals at once, `CAUSE_NO_ACCESS` — the erasure of what is left of the account that the registry names (the 2026-11-10 amendment to R04), `unimplemented` — an offer to reload (section 3.7); a failure of these actions does not replace the refusal: the screen learns its cause;
  - sign-in: the ID token in `Authorization` for every method except `GetManifest`, `GetSkillGraph`, `GetTechniques`, `GetSolvingSteps` and `GetLegalTexts`; `unauthenticated` without a `Refusal` — one token refresh and one retry, and a refusal with a cause gets no refresh: the cause is read first;
  - retry: `unavailable` without a cause, `CAUSE_PACKAGE_UNKNOWN` and a network failure — after pauses of 0.5, 1, 2 and 4 s, with the same message, and so with the same idempotency key; `CAUSE_STILL_GOING` waits for the first request up to 30 s — as long as `api` lets it run, — with the pause from `Retry-After`, otherwise, after the last pause, 4 s each. A call that its deadline or its caller cut off is not retried.

  `app` has no headers of its own, and connect-web sends only the allowed ones — a test holds this.
- **A failure** is an `ApiError` with the field `failure`: `refused` with the cause, its data and its code, `offline`, `sign-in`, `fresh-sign-in` with the allowed age of the sign-in, `account-gone`, `no-access`, `stale`, `invalid`, `forbidden` and `unknown` with the code. A cause that the build does not know, the client reads by the error code with any code — and with `unauthenticated` it refreshes the token, as without a cause, — and an unfamiliar outcome as an AI failure. A token that cannot be got is none: the request goes without it, and then comes the refresh.
- **Keys and clocks.** The idempotency key is `crypto.randomUUID()` per action; the screen holds it until the action is done. With a request to the platform AI goes the clock offset, and with the own key — the key and not the offset.
- **The words** are common to all screens: an AI failure and the platform AI's limit — one neutral phrase (R04 spec), the quota — with the time when the next family day begins, in the screen's language, AI not admitted, the limit and the refusal of the own key, no consent, a version withdrawn, "This isn't there any more", an attempt closed, no access, "Sign in again" and "Sign in again to do this". The causes of their own screens — PIN, invite code, country, the lesson's segments, "Explain differently" — the tasks of those screens supply with words, and until then it is the common string `common.unknown`.
- **The screens** take the client through `useApi()`, and a read through `useRequest()`: loading, an answer or a failure, and `retry()`. An action with a key the screen calls itself, on a tap. `Boot` makes the client once per `api` of the file, and takes the tokens and `fetch` from its properties on each call: new objects of them do not make a new client and do not repeat requests. Until T22 nobody is signed in. Each method of the client is the same function on every read, and `useRequest` does not show the answer of a former call as the answer of a new one.

— *Why:* the author's answers. The contract's rules — the cause before the token refresh, one key for all retries, retrying "for a short while" — are its own, and a server-state library does not know them, and the cache of open views lies in IndexedDB by digest anyway (R16). The words of a cause depend on the screen on which it comes, and writing them before the screen means writing them twice.

— *Rejected:*
- TanStack Query — a new dependency in what ships and in `NOTICE`, whose retries and cache would have to be turned off or made to obey the contract's rules;
- gRPC-Web — `api` speaks Connect (R11 spec, R83 spec); JSON on the wire — connect-es reads an unfamiliar enum value in JSON as empty ([the contracts text](../spec/contracts/README.md), section 9);
- a retry without a limit — "for a short while" ([the contracts text](../spec/contracts/README.md), section 15);
- the words of all 38 causes now — the argument above.

— *Consequence:* T21 takes the client for `CheckPackages` and makes its own Connect GET transport for the open views; T22 gives the tokens of Firebase Auth, the sign-in window on `sign-in` and `fresh-sign-in` and the words of its causes; T24–T28 call `AttemptService` and `LearningService` with keys and `aiFields`. — [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.3, 3.7, 3.8 and 4; RUN.md, T20.

— *Amendment 2026-11-12:* **the words of the quota name the day when it comes back — "today", "tomorrow" or a date, by the device's calendar, — and the block "Tutor" names the quota and the platform AI's limit both under a rung's fallback text and after a correct answer without words.** The executor decided it in the planning of T49, and the author looks at it. In detail — R37.

## Sign-in, the account and the network of the local run

**R19. `app` signs in by the Firebase Auth window behind the narrow interface of the sign-in service, which `Boot` sets up only after the sign-in project is checked against the registry. `app` reads the account by `GetAccount` at the start and after every sign-in, and by its answer erases on the device everything that does not belong to this account before anything opens; with no account, an invite code with the statement "I am 18"; with no owner profile, a profile of the category `adult` with a pseudonym and a country; and signing out names what will be lost and erases everything of the account. The container of `app` is also on the Docker network `novatrail-api`, and the Vite proxy reaches the local `api` at its container's address.** The author's answers of 2026-11-10 in the planning of T22: the network, by the recommendation, and T22 as one task; the rest are the plan's proposals, which the author approved with it (RUN.md, T22, «Решено автором»). The design is in [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.2, 3.7 and 3.10, "Code", and the sources: section 4.
- **The sign-in service** is `src/signin/service.ts`: who is signed in — the user's ID at the sign-in service, `null` or "not read yet" —, the sign-in window, signing out, the client's tokens (R18) and the window's language. In the browser `firebase.ts` gives it: `initializeApp` with the file's parameters, explicitly, `initializeAuth` with `indexedDBLocalPersistence` alone and `browserPopupRedirectResolver`, `signInWithPopup` with the Google provider and `languageCode`, the screen's language at each change of it. The window's error codes: `popup-closed-by-user`, `cancelled-popup-request` and `user-cancelled` — the window was closed, with no line; `popup-blocked` — the browser did not open the window; `network-request-failed` — no connection; `unauthorized-domain`, `invalid-api-key` and `operation-not-allowed` — the project does not accept this copy, with a line for whoever deployed it; the rest — "The sign-in didn't go through".
- **The order of the start.** `Boot` makes the service once per page, and only when the outcome of the check is `same` or `erased` (R16): `initializeAuth` would read the previous project's session as this one's. The outcome `blocked` is the screen "The app is open in another tab with the previous sign-in", `failed` is "The data on this device can't be read", and the sign-in is not set up in this tab. The property `signIn`, a factory of the service, in the tests one of their own, has replaced the property `tokens` of `Boot`, there since T20.
- **The session** is `src/account/session.ts`, without the DOM. Once there is a sign-in, the registry remembers `projectId` and `apiKey` (section 3.7), then `GetAccount`:
  - an account — `accountSeen`: the registry's previous account is erased, and so is everything the registry still holds for erasure, and if another tab holds the database, the account does not open: the screen "Another account's data on this device can't be erased yet" and "Try again"; then `profilesListed`: the databases of profiles not in the list are erased too, but the app does not hold their `blocked`: the next start erases them; then the owner profile or its creation;
  - "no access" — the invite code; what was left of the registry's account, the client erased (R18);
  - no connection — nothing is erased, and "Try again" asks again;
  - a sign-in that cannot be refreshed, and an account that does not exist — the sign-in screen;
  - another UID — everything anew, and the answer for the previous one is dropped, as is the answer of a session already stopped. A window that signed in as the same user asks for the account itself, and one that signed in as a new user waits until the service tells of it: one `GetAccount` per sign-in.

  The checks of packages (T21) begin once there is an account and end with it: before the sign-in `app` has no token.
- **The account and the profile.** `CreateAccount` sends the code as typed, without spaces at the edges, and `adultStated` only when the box "I am 18 or older" is ticked (R118 spec): without the box there is no request. `CAUSE_CODE_NOT_ACCEPTED` is a line at the field, `CAUSE_ALREADY_EXISTS` is `GetAccount` again. `CreateOwnerProfile` sends a pseudonym of 1 to 30 Unicode characters after trimming the edges, which is checked before the request, and a country from a list of 249 ISO 3166-1 alpha-2 codes, with the names of `Intl.DisplayNames` in the screen's language. Which countries the deployment does not accept, `api` decides: `CAUSE_COUNTRY_NOT_ACCEPTED` is a line at the country field, and `app` has no list of closed countries of its own.
- **Choosing the learner**: the owner profile, without a PIN: there are no children's profiles in E2 (section 3.6). The chosen one is in memory alone; "Switch learner" goes back to the choice. At the account's first sign-in on this device, a line that the learners' work is kept here, and the button `persist()` (section 3.2).
- **Signing out** is a screen of its own: what signing out will erase and, for each profile, how many unfinished attempts are on this device, "Sign out and erase" and "Cancel". First the sign-in service's `signOut()`, then `eraseAccount`, as on "account gone". If the service did not sign out, nothing is erased — the same user would open the account again, now without the data —, and the sign-out screen says the sign-out did not go through. What the browser did not erase, the next start erases, and the sign-in screen says so in a line. While the erasure runs, a new sign-in starts nothing, and the sign-out screen, if the account went otherwise — a sign-out in another tab, "account gone" —, closes, and the next account does not open on it. "Account gone" erases each time it comes, and two refusals at once erase once.
- **The words**: those of the screens of the sign-in, the invite code, the profile, the choice and the sign-out; and the language buttons move from the home screen to the sign-in screen (R17). The glossary is section 3.8.
- **The network of the local run.** `api` in its devcontainer is on the network `novatrail-api`, publishes no port outside and listens on all interfaces (section 4). The container of `app` is on two networks, `novatrail-app` and `novatrail-api`, the second `--network` in `runArgs`; `host-network.sh` sets up both with one MTU, so `app` starts without `api` too. The proxy goes to `NOVATRAIL_API=http://<address of the api container>:8080`, `hostname -i` in the terminal of `api` gives the address, and `API_APP_ORIGIN` of `api` is not set: the origin of `app` needs no CORS. This is how the devcontainer of `app` differs from the template of `spec`.

— *Why:* the author's answers. A service behind an interface gives tests without Firebase and one place where it is decided when the session is read. Erasing by the answer of `GetAccount` before the app opens is the rule of R04 with its 2026-11-10 amendment and of [the contracts text](../spec/contracts/README.md), section 15. The second network opens nothing beyond Docker and needs no change of `api`.

— *Rejected:*
- `getAuth` — it looks for the parameters and the emulator in the environment by itself (section 3.7);
- the sign-in service before the check of the project — the previous project's session would be read as the new one's;
- the port of `api` on the host and `host.docker.internal` — a port published on `0.0.0.0` opens `api` to the local network, and one on the host's `127.0.0.1` cannot be reached from the container;
- a shared network of all NovaTrail containers — a change of the template of `spec` for one case; the name of the `api` container on the network is the row «в реестр spec» В47 (table 3 of RUN.md);
- a list of closed countries of its own in `app` — the deployment holds it, and it would drift from the list of `api`;
- T22 as two tasks — the author's answer.

— *Consequence:* part of Д8 is closed — the sign-in window in Chromium under the CSP header, with the network log, while the devices, iPad and Android, wait for E3. `@firebase/app` keeps its own database `firebase-heartbeat-database` on the device and sends its records to Google with the requests to Firebase Auth — the row «в реестр spec» В48 (section 3.7). A fresh sign-in by the window comes with deletions, E4. T23 opens "My subjects" under the chosen learner (`useLearner()`). — [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.2, 3.7, 3.8, 3.10 and 4; RUN.md, T22.

— *Amendment 2026-11-11:* `CreateAccount` also carries the versions of `privacy-policy` and `terms-of-use` that the owner accepted: the invite screen shows the texts and asks for the tick (R27). `spec` recorded the network of the local run by the rows «в реестр spec»: on loopback, `api` is at the origin of `app` itself, behind the proxy (Д177 of RUN.md of `spec`; [the part `deployment/`](../spec/deployment/README.md), section 5) — the answer to В46; the container's name on its network, `--network-alias`, the devcontainer template will give by task T115 of `spec` (Д195 of RUN.md of `spec`) — the answer to В47, but on v0.15.0 it is not there yet, so until then the address of `api` is still `hostname -i`. So it was recorded in R26 at T32. — [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.2 and 3.7.

— *Amendment 2026-11-14:* `API_APP_ORIGIN` of `api` in the local run is the origin of `app` on loopback, `http://127.0.0.1:5173`, as README of `api` has it, and not unset as above: behind the proxy the requests need no CORS, but the variable rejects nothing (Д237 `spec`). The template of `spec` v0.17.1 gives the container its name on its own network, and `app`'s has `--network-alias=app` since R43 (T115; Д195 `spec`); `api`'s container gets the name `api` by its own move, T74 of `api` (Д31 of `api`), and until then the proxy goes by `hostname -i` (Д21). The container also mounts the volume `novatrail-dev-token` at `/home/vscode/.novatrail-token`, only to read it (R43), and so differs from the template by the second network and this volume. — `.devcontainer/`; [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.7; DEBT.md, Д21.

— *Amendment 2026-11-14:* the proxy goes to `http://api:8080` by default, since `api`'s container has the name `api` by T74 of `api`, and `NOVATRAIL_API` names another address: not by `hostname -i` as above (Д21 closed). The local build signs in by `api`'s development token, with no window, when `just dev-app` gives one, and otherwise by the window (R44). — [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.7.

**R20. `app` opens the task screen from the start of an attempt, `AttemptStart`: in E2 by the open attempt that `GetAccount` named, and by "Try it again" after its end, and the address `#/task` does not name the attempt. The canvas, with the items with options under it, is on the left, the tutor's panel on the right from 64rem and above the canvas on a narrow screen, and "Check" on a line of its own under them. The verdict stands at the chosen option and at the item, as a word and a sign, and "Correct" replaces the panel of steps with the outcome: "Correct", the task's solution, "Try it again" and "Back to My subjects".** The author's answers of 2026-11-10 in the planning of T24: the entry, the layout and the verdict, all by the recommendations, and T24 as one task; the rest are the plan's proposals, which the author approved with it (RUN.md, T24, «Решено автором»). The design is in [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.2, 3.3 and 3.10, "Code".
- **The entry.** `BeginAttempt` begins an attempt only by an engine choice, a place in a lesson or a past attempt, and only `ChooseNext` writes choices — T40 of `api` (section 4). So in E2, until T27 and T28, the task screen is opened by an open attempt outside a lesson that `GetAccount` named: "My subjects" shows "There is a task in progress" and "Continue the task" —, and by "Try it again" with `previous_attempt_id`. The lesson player, T27, leads a lesson's attempt. The ID of the current attempt is in memory alone (`src/task/AttemptContext.tsx`), and the screen `#/task` without an attempt leads home (R14). An attempt that ended stays on the screen with its outcome until the learner leaves, and "My subjects" no longer offers it.
- **What the screen reads.** `GetOpenAttempt` is the start of the attempt: the task's open view by `module_format_version` within the build's window, otherwise an offer to reload (`OpenViews.parseView`, R44 spec), and the package digest, by which the manifest, the solving steps and the technique catalog are taken from the shared cache (section 3.2). What this device wrote in the attempt — the writing history and the step state — comes from the profile's database; with no record, a new record with a clean sheet, and the step state with the options of the last submission, the number of wrong answers and the step's techniques, which `api` keeps. Each change is written at once, and a record that did not go in is the line "Your work isn't saved on this device".
- **Refusals.** `CAUSE_ATTEMPT_CLOSED` with a close reason is the screen "This task is already over" with the reason in words and "Try it again"; the attempt's work on the device is erased, and for "Not checked" `app` also asks `CheckPackages` (T21). `CAUSE_NOT_FOUND` of `GetOpenAttempt` — home. No connection — a screen with "Try again", the rest — the words of T20 with "Try again".
- **The layout.** From 64rem, two columns: the canvas and the items, the panel; narrower, one: the panel, the canvas, the items, "Check". An item of the sheet is a `<fieldset>`: its text or "Item n", and a slot `choice` is radio buttons, with "Answer n" in an item of several slots; the keyboard is the native one. In the mode "On my own" there is no panel until the task is solved.
- **"Check"** is `aria-disabled` while some slot holds nothing, with the line "Choose an answer in each item"; for a task with a slot that is not `choice` it is closed until T25, the submission of handwriting. The idempotency key is one per submission of these options, the same on its retry, and another option is another action and a new key (R18).
- **The verdict** stands at the chosen option, outside its `<label>` and as the radio button's description, and at the item's text: "✓ Correct", "• Not yet", "• Can't read this", "• Not checked", and an unknown code is a common line. The summary "Not right yet: n items of m" is `role="status"`. A changed option removes the verdict of its slot and item: it was the verdict of another answer.
- **"Correct".** The attempt is closed: its work on the device is erased — a choice has no work for the correct answer words —, the panel is replaced by the outcome, the solution `SolvedTask` — paragraphs and pictures by `blob:` with their `alt` —, and the options can no longer be pressed. The screen does not show the keys of `SolvedTask`: for a choice the key is the option itself, already marked "Correct".
- **The steps** are by R05: the strip is a list with `aria-current="step"` and the word of the place — "done", "now", "later" —, the steps done are buttons to go back; the step's name is an `h2` that takes the focus when the step changes; `description`, the chips — buttons `aria-pressed`, `givens` and `unknown` apart —, the cards of techniques, the line of the condition and "Step done" under `aria-disabled`. `BeginStep` once per step whose `gives` gives techniques, only in the mode "by steps"; a failure asks again on the return to the step. The step state is `src/task/steps.ts`, without the DOM: the mode, the place, the furthest step, the marks, the techniques, the chosen options and the number of wrong answers.
- **The blink** is `src/task/pause.ts`: 6 s with no stroke and no choice while "Check" is open — one blink, three flashes of 1 s each; again only after a new action; under `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce`, a still outline.

— *Why:* the author's answers. Without `ChooseNext` there is nothing else to begin an attempt on `api` with, and the open attempt is the very path by which the learner returns to the task after a restart. Two columns keep the sheet in place while the panel changes, and a verdict beside the option does not make the learner look for what it is about.

— *Rejected:*
- beginning a task through `ChooseNext` already in T24 — until T40 of `api` it answers `unimplemented`, and part of T28 would move into T24;
- a debug entry with the ID of a choice — code of the development build that is not in the release, and a row put into the database of `api` by hand;
- the panel above the canvas at any width — on a wide screen the sheet goes down;
- a separate screen of the outcome — the work on the canvas goes away from the solution;
- "Check" stuck to the bottom of a narrow screen, though the author approved it: in the accessibility check the line covered the chip under it at 320 px — a target that another target covers, `axe-core` rejects (WCAG 2.5.8), and the focus would go under the line (2.4.11). "Check" stands right under the items, and on a narrow screen it is one scroll away;
- hidden headings "Tutor" and "Steps" — the panel and the strip are named by `aria-label`, and the step's name is an `h2`;
- "Step done" and "Check" turned off by `disabled` — the focus would fall to the page, as in T23.

— *Consequence:* "Hint" comes with T26, and until then it is not on the task screen, though section 3.3 asks for it always; the submission of handwriting and the marks — T25; a lesson's task — T27; a new task by the engine choice — T28. The check of the screen by hand on a local `api` moves to T30: `api` has neither `ChooseNext` nor a module of format 0.4 yet. The canvas under the CSP on the built page was checked in Chromium — part of Д9. — [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.1, 3.2, 3.3, 3.8, 3.9, 3.10 and 4; RUN.md, T24.

**R21. `app` sends handwriting as the canvas gives it: on "Check", `exportWork()`, the submission into the profile's database with its context, and `SubmitWork` with the crop, with one key for this work and the same on a retry. The verdict of a handwriting slot stands at the item's text with "Read: …" or a reason for "can't read", the tutor's words in the block "Tutor" at the top of the panel, and the marks as boxes on the tutor's layer of the canvas. "Not yet" asks at once for the rung's words with the same work, and "Correct" keeps the work of the correct submission until the correct answer words.** The author's answer of 2026-11-10 in the planning of T25: where the verdict, the words and the marks are, by the recommendation; the rest are the plan's proposals, which the author approved with it (RUN.md, T25, «Решено автором»). The design is in [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.2 and 3.3, "Code".
- **The submission.** "Check" for a task with handwriting is open when every slot holds something (`filled` of the element and the chosen options), and closed with the line "Write an answer on the sheet". On it: `exportWork()`, `addSubmission` with the length of the history — if the record did not go in, the line "Your work isn't saved on this device" —, and `SubmitWork`: for a sheet, the PNG of the crop, for a workbook, the crops of the boxes with their places, as bytes; with them the options and the clock offset, since the platform AI reads the work (R18). A retry of an unanswered submission goes with the same key and the same work, and a new stroke or option is a new action and a new key.
- **The answer.** The verdicts are as for a choice (R20), and under an item of handwriting, "Read: {value}" or the reason: `blank`, `notation`, `illegible`, `ambiguous`, `no_final_answer`, `several_final_answers`; an unknown one is a common line. The outcome of the AI — a failure, the quota, the limit, "not admitted", a refusal of the key — is a line at "Check" in the words of R18, and the slots the AI did not read are "Not checked".
- **The rung's words.** A wrong answer — `wrong_answers` grew — and `app` at once calls `GetRungWords` with this number, the same work, the own key and the clock offset. The words go into the block "Tutor", with the `lang` of the explanation language, and the live line says only "The tutor's words came" (section 3.9). While the models' words are not admitted, `api` gives the rung's fallback text with the outcome "not admitted": these are words, and then there is no line of the outcome; an outcome without words is a line in the block. The refusals "answer not current", "no rung" and "no more words" show nothing, a closed attempt is the end screen (R20). After a restart the rung's words come from `OpenAttempt.rung`.
- **The marks** come only with the rung's words (R97 spec): `crop_box` for a sheet is a box `{ box }` in thousandths of the crop, `answer_slot` for a workbook is `{ cell }`; a mark without a place or of an unknown kind does not count. The name is "Tutor's mark n of m", and for a workbook also ": line l, box b". The element gets them with the context of that submission, which the database kept.
- **"Correct".** `attemptEnded` with the number of the correct submission: its work goes to `correctWork`, and the rest is erased (the second 2026-11-10 amendment to R04). Then `GetCorrectAnswerWords` with this work, and when the answer has come or the request has ended, `wordsCame`. The words, if they came, go into the outcome with `lang`; for now `api` does not give them (В47 of `api`).

— *Why:* the author's answer. The verdict beside what it judged, as for a choice; the words where the tutor speaks of the steps; the marks where the strokes they outline are. `app` asks for the rung's words because the server does not keep the crop, and only the device can send it once more (R97 spec).

— *Rejected:*
- a strip of the outcome under the canvas — the author's answer: on a narrow screen it pushes the items away, and the words stand far from the steps;
- removing the marks with each new stroke — the canvas keeps a box on the strokes and dims it while the learner writes in it (R26, R27 canvas); a new submission removes the marks. So the plan of T25 was corrected during its execution;
- giving `marks` as a JSX property — a mark that the element rejects would bring the screen down with a `TypeError` at rendering. `app` sets `marks` after rendering, when `task` and `history` are already on the element (R36 spec), and drops a rejected one;
- building the rung's fallback text on the device from the manifest's `rung_templates` — the trap text comes first, and traps do not come to the device (section 3.3; R05 spec);
- a button "Ask for words" after "Not yet" — an extra tap: the rung's question is part of the answer to a wrong answer (R82 spec).

— *Consequence:* Д4 is closed for the mouse — the measurement in Chromium is written into section 3.2, and the pen and the finger wait for devices in E3. The check of handwriting by hand on a local `api` is T30: `api` has no module of format 0.4 yet, and the reading of handwriting is a paid call of Anthropic, the first in T42 of `api`. — [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.2, 3.3, 3.8, 3.10 and 4; RUN.md, T25.

**R22. "Hint" always stands beside "Check" and asks `GetHint`: until the first wrong answer it shows in the block "Hint" the givens and the unknown, the task's techniques and its leading question, and after it the words of the current rung once more, without the AI. "Explain differently" is a button in the block "Tutor", once per rung. "Show the solution" is visible to a learner of the category `adult` from the start of the attempt, to a child from the fourth wrong answer, and the showing asks for confirmation: it ends the attempt.** Task T26 was run without planning: what to do, R82 spec and section 3.3 decided, and here is how it fell on the screen of R20 and R21. The design is in [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.3, "Code".
- **"Hint"** is `GetHint`, without a key and without the work.
  - Until the first wrong answer, the answer is the IDs of the task's techniques and the leading question. The block "Hint" at the top of the panel shows as a list "What we know" and "What to find" from the open view, the cards of techniques from the catalog and the question, all with the module's `lang`. If there is neither a technique nor a question, the line "This task has nothing more to hint".
  - After it the answer is the words of the current rung, and they go into the block "Tutor", as the rung's words after a check do.
  - The open attempt keeps what was given — `requested_technique_ids` and `requested_leading_question` —, and until the first wrong answer the screen shows them from there, without a request.
- **"Explain differently"** is `ExplainDifferently` with the number of the wrong answer whose words the learner is reading, a key and the clock offset.
  - The new words take the place of the previous ones. The outcome `CAUSE_NO_OTHER_WORDS` gives the line "The tutor has no other words for this", and another outcome gives its words (R18).
  - Any answer spends the right on the rung. The refusals "already asked", "answer not current" and "no rung" spend it too: the button is gone. The refusals of the quota, the limit and admission leave the right, and the button stays with the line of the refusal. A network failure is retried with the same key.
  - A new wrong answer is a new rung and again its own "Explain differently". The open attempt tells `explained_differently` of the current rung.
- **"Show the solution"** first asks "Once the solution is shown, the task is over" with "Show it" and "Cancel"; the focus moves to "Show it", and after "Cancel" it comes back.
  - Then `ShowSolution` as the learner's action with a key. The answer closes the attempt: its work on the device is erased, and the panel is the outcome "Solution shown" with the solution.
  - The refusal "adult needed" is the line "A grown-up needs to help"; "ladder not passed" is "The solution can be shown after a few more answers"; a closed attempt is the end screen (R20).
  - The adult action behind a PIN is E4, with the section for adults.

— *Why:* R82 spec and section 3.3. "Hint" and "Check" are actions on the task, and stand together; "Explain differently" is about the tutor's words, and stands at them. Showing the solution ends the attempt with no undo, so it is asked once more — sending an answer by "Check" asks for no confirmation, but its verdict can be put right by the next submission (R11).

— *Rejected:*
- «Объясни иначе» on the button — in Russian the learner's screens do not address the learner (section 3.8): «Объяснить иначе», as «Проверить»;
- showing the solution with one tap — the attempt would end from a chance tap on a tablet;
- a hint before a wrong answer without a request, from the open view alone — the technique and the question are closed, and `api` records what was given (R108 spec).

— *Consequence:* the row of the conversation of the first slice — the buttons "Hint" and "Explain differently" — is ready (section 3.10). Calling an adult for a child, and showing the solution by an adult action — E4. — [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.3, 3.8 and 3.10; RUN.md, T26.

**R23. `app` opens a lesson by a lesson run already begun — "Continue the lesson" in "My subjects" by `open_lesson_runs` of `GetAccount`, through `GetLessonRun` —, and a new lesson run by the engine choice, `OpenLesson`, is ready for T28; the address `#/lesson` does not name the lesson run. The lesson's header is its title and the strip of segments: chips of 44 × 44 with a number and the sign ✓ on a passed one, wrapping to a second line, and from 64rem also with the word of the kind. A lesson's task is the task screen under the header, without "Try it again"; the place moves by the `next` of the answer about the task's end at once, and "Continue" on the last segment is `CompleteLesson`.** The author's answers of 2026-11-10 in the planning of T27: the strip of segments on a narrow screen and the entry, both by the recommendations; the rest are the plan's proposals, which the author approved with it (RUN.md, T27, «Решено автором»). The design is in [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.4, "Code".
- **The entry.** The learner's lesson runs begun — `open_lesson_runs` from `GetAccount` — are held by the tab's memory per profile, as the open attempt is (R20); a lesson run that ended leaves the list. The block "A lesson in progress" above "Connected" names each by the lesson's title from the state on the device, with the module's `lang`, and with no state as "A lesson: {module}", where the module's name is a placeholder element with its own `lang`. With no lesson run chosen, `#/lesson` goes home at its own place in the history.
- **The key of the lesson's state** is the lesson's full ID, the package digest and, beside them, the ID of the lesson run (`learning.proto`). `LessonRun` does not carry the full ID: `app` builds it from the module ID of the manifest and the lesson's ID by [the format text](../spec/format/README.md), section 10, and with no manifest takes `LessonRunRef.lesson`.
- **The strip of segments.** A list of buttons by places; a button's name is the segment's number and kind, exactly its text (2.5.3): the word of the kind is hidden on a narrow screen by `display: none`, and the theme draws the sign ✓ and does not read it into the name. "passed", "now" and "later" are the button's description, and on a segment that does not open, in their place, why: "This task is over" or "Not open yet"; pressing it says so in a line of the screen. Under the strip, the heading `h2` "Worked example · 2 of 4", to which the focus goes when the segment changes (2.4.3).
- **A lesson's task** is `BeginAttempt` with the segment of the lesson run and a key, the same on a retry; only a refusal resets the key. An attempt opened at the place, `app` takes from `LessonRun.open_attempt`. The task screen stands under the lesson's header without its own screen, "Try it again" and "Back to My subjects"; a closed attempt is a line of the segment. An attempt closed by "left" or "abandoned" does not end the segment — "Begin the task again" —, and its other end is a repeated `GetLessonRun`.
- **What's next.** `next.segment` moves the place at once, while the learner still sees the task's outcome; `segments_ended` leaves the place on the last segment, and "Continue" on it is "Lesson complete"; an empty `next` is a repeated `GetLessonRun`. The refusal `CAUSE_SEGMENT_UNAVAILABLE` leads on if the segment ended by itself, and otherwise to the first task not ended, even if before the place; a refusal without places is a repeated `GetLessonRun`, not "nothing ended". An attempt that `api` no longer knows, the task screen in a lesson hands to the player and does not lead home. The quota, the limit, "not admitted" and an answer without an attempt are a line and "Begin the task again", and the lesson run stays open (Д26 of `api`).
- **The end.** `CompleteLesson` on "Continue" on the last segment; a network failure is a line, and "Continue" sends it again, while the client itself retries `unavailable`. `CAUSE_SEGMENTS_NOT_ENDED` opens the first task segment not ended with the line "The lesson has a task to finish". Any end of the lesson run — by an answer or by `CAUSE_RUN_ENDED` — erases the lesson's state and removes the lesson run from the list; "Not checked" also calls `CheckPackages`. The end screen is the lesson's title, "Lesson complete" or a neutral phrase of the reason, and "Back to My subjects".

— *Why:* without the engine choice (T28) there is nothing to begin a new lesson run with, and `api` itself names those begun. Chips with numbers keep the strip at 320 px to one or two lines and leave the kind of each segment in its name. The place moves by `next` at once, as R06 asks: otherwise a restart after a correct answer would open a task that has ended.

— *Rejected:*
- the line "Worked example · 2 of 4" and unfolding the list of segments on a narrow screen — one more tap to go back (the author's answer);
- a column of segments with words — the header above each task of the lesson grows with the number of segments (the author's answer);
- the address `#/lesson/<choice_id>` in the development build until T28 — code that is not in the release (the author's answer);
- a chip's name longer than its text, "2. Worked example, passed" — the visible text "2 Worked example" would not be in the name whole (2.5.3);
- the signs of steps passed on the strip of segments — «пройден» agrees with «шагом», while «звено» is neuter: the strip of segments has words of its own, «пройдено», «сейчас», «дальше»;
- `NOT_SUITABLE` in the common table of the words of reasons in the lesson's words — a task outside a lesson gets this reason too.

— *Consequence:* Д26 of `api` — a lesson whose task by handwriting is not admitted to the AI is stuck before "Lesson complete"; the screen says the refusal, and back and leaving work. The button to the engine choice on the end screen, and the entry by the engine choice — T28; a lesson by hand on a local `api` — T30. — [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.2, 3.4, 3.8, 3.9 and 3.10; RUN.md, T27.

**R24. `app` leads to what `engine` chose with one button: "Next lesson" or "Next task" by the choice that the answer about the end of an attempt outside a lesson, or of a lesson run, gave, and without it "What's next", which asks `ChooseNext`. In "My subjects" with no open attempt stands "What's next", and beside "Continue the task", "Something else", which first says that the task in progress will close and cannot be gone back to. A lesson choice opens the player by `OpenLesson`, a task choice the task screen by `BeginAttempt`, and "nothing to give" is the line "There is nothing more to offer for now", with the outcome if the AI is closed.** Task T28 was run without planning: what to do, R99 spec, the comments of `LearningService` and section 3.4 decided, and here is how it fell on the screens of R20 and R23. The design is in [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.4, "Code".
- **Where the button is.** On the outcome of a task outside a lesson, under the solution, before "Try it again", which has become secondary; on the screen "This task is already over", secondary, after "Try it again"; on the end screen of a lesson, before "Back to My subjects"; in "My subjects", "What's next" above the lists, when there is no open attempt outside a lesson.
- **A choice from the answer.** `next.choice` of the answer of `SubmitWork` with "Correct" and of the answer of `ShowSolution`, `RunEnd.choice` of the answer of `CompleteLesson` and of the refusal `CAUSE_RUN_ENDED` — a button by what the choice gives, without a request. A choice "nothing to give" from the answer is a line without a button. An end without a choice is "What's next".
- **"What's next" and "Something else"** are `ChooseNext` with the action's key and an empty skill: the skill map is E3. The answer leads at once to what the choice gives. An open attempt outside a lesson is closed, `CLOSE_REASON_LEFT`, both by `ChooseNext` and by beginning a task by a choice: before each such action, the question "The task in progress closes, and it can't be gone back to", wherever the button stands, and after it `app` erases the attempt's work on the device and forgets it as open, as at the end of an attempt (R04). A lesson by a choice does not close the attempt and does not ask. With an open attempt and no choice at hand, the button is "Something else".
- **A task by a choice** is `BeginAttempt` with `choice_id` and a key of its own. The refusal `CAUSE_ALREADY_EXISTS` — the choice has already begun an attempt: `app` reads the profile's open attempt from `GetAccount` and opens it. The task screen `#/task` takes the new attempt even when it was begun from that screen itself, and the player `#/lesson` takes a new chosen lesson, with a new player.
- **The keys** are as in R22: one key per action and the same on a retry, and only a refusal resets it.
- **The reason of the choice** the screen does not name: the answer to the learner does not carry it (concepts 12 spec).

— *Why:* one button with the name of where it leads is clearer than a general "Next", which is, besides, a button of the canvas element (R06). "Something else" closes the attempt with no way back, so it is asked once more, as showing the solution is (R22), and "What's next" with no open attempt closes nothing and asks nothing.

— *Rejected:*
- one general button "Next" on every screen — it would clash with the "Next" of a worked example, and the learner would not know whether a lesson or a task is ahead;
- "Something else" inside a lesson too, beside its task — "Leave the lesson" is enough for the first slice, and the lesson goes on from the same place;
- moving on to the choice by itself after "Correct" — the learner is still reading the solution (product 7 spec).

— *Consequence:* the path of the first app — a lesson, a task and the choice of the next — is ready for T30. The skill map with the choice of a skill, and consolidation in a lesson — E3 (R107 spec). — [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.4, 3.8 and 3.10; RUN.md, T28.

**R25. A release of the build of `app` is one archive `app_<version>.tar.gz`, the only file of `dist/`: at its root `index.html`, `assets/`, `deployment.schema.json` and `csp.json` by the rollout layout of `infra`, without `deployment.json`, and also `NOTICE` and `LICENSE`. `scripts/release.ts` packs it with its own code, to the same bytes on every run, and the shared `release.yml` of `.github` v0.1.22 with `RELEASE_LINE` 0.1 signs it. Until the first release every run is a dry run with `publish: false`: it builds, signs and checks, publishing nothing.** The author's answers of 2026-11-11 in the planning of T29: `NOTICE` and `LICENSE` inside the archive, and the dry run without publishing, both by the recommendations; the rest are the plan's proposals, which the author approved with it (RUN.md, T29, «Решено автором»). The design is in [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.1 and 3.7, and the sources: section 4.
- **The contents.** Everything from `build/` and, beside it, the repository's `NOTICE` and `LICENSE`. The bundle does not keep the packages' license comments, so the notices of third-party code — React, Firebase, `canvas` and others — travel in the same archive: a copy taken on its own carries them, and the site serves them at `/NOTICE` and `/LICENSE`. The rollout of `infra` does not reject extra files and puts them all out (R17 infra), and `spec` records the layout as an agreement by the row «в реестр spec» В49 before the first release.
- **Packing.** ustar, entries of the form `./index.html` by name, the time, the owner and the group 0, the mode `0644` for files and `0755` for folders; a long path is split into the ustar fields at the first slash at which both fit. `gzip` of level 9 without a name and a time, and the byte of the system in its header "unknown", `0xff`: otherwise it would depend on the machine Node was built on. The recipe rejects a link in the build and a file of the build named `NOTICE` or `LICENSE`, and rejects a version not of the form `vX.Y.Z` already before the build.
- **The check**, before writing and in `ci-test`: only files and folders, with no path outside; at the root exactly five files and `assets/` with a file; `deployment.json` nowhere; the time, the owner and the mode as above; `NOTICE` and `LICENSE` byte for byte as in the repository; the lower layer of the CSP first in `<head>` after `<meta charset>`. `ci-test` also builds the tree a second time into its own folder and compares the bytes of the two archives: so a non-reproducible build itself is caught too, not only one of the packer.
- **`release.yml`** runs on `workflow_run` of CI on `main`, only after a green push not from a fork, on `pull_request` from a branch of this repository and not from Dependabot, and on the button, without a path filter of its own: a run of a fork and of Dependabot gets no OIDC token, without which keyless signing does not go, and the dry run checks Dependabot's changes on `main` after the merge; `paths` — the code without the tests, the page, `csp.json`, the part of `spec` in `parts/`, the scripts of the build and the archive, the configuration, the packages, `.npmrc`, `NOTICE`, `LICENSE`, the recipe, the stage `toolchain` and the file itself.

— *Why:* the notices must go with the code that is distributed, and what is distributed is the archive, and the site comes from it as well. The license texts, read on 2026-11-11 in `node_modules` and in the repository's `LICENSE`: Apache-2.0, §4(d), — “If the Work includes a "NOTICE" text file as part of its distribution, then any Derivative Works that You distribute must include a readable copy of the attribution notices contained within such NOTICE file”; MIT (`react`) — "The above copyright notice and this permission notice shall be included in all copies or substantial portions of the Software"; ISC (`idb`) — "provided that the above copyright notice and this permission notice appear in all copies". A packer of its own does not depend on which `tar` is on the machine, and gives the same bytes: the check by sha256 in the rollout list and the signature rest on them. The dry run before the first release builds and signs as a real run does, but creates neither a tag nor a release, until the repository keeps its releases immutable (Д54 in table 4 of RUN.md of `spec`).

— *Rejected:*
- `NOTICE` and `LICENSE` beside the archive in the release — a layout without a row in `spec`, but the site would serve the code without the notices, and a copy of the archive would not carry them (the author's answer);
- the notices only on the legal pages of E3 — until then the release would distribute the code without the notices;
- publishing on `main` at once, as `canvas` does — the merge of T29 would release v0.1.0 before Д54 and T31 (the author's answer);
- the system's `tar` with flags of reproducibility, as `sim` has — the recipe would depend on which `tar` is on the machine of whoever builds, and the check of the archive reads tar with its own code anyway;
- `SHA256SUMS` beside the archive — the rollout list of `infra` and the release's attestation hold the archive's sha256, and the file would only repeat them.

— *Consequence:* the row «в реестр spec» В49 — the layout with `NOTICE` and `LICENSE`. The first release and `publish` on `main` — T31, after the author's step Д54. — [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.1, 3.7 and 4; RUN.md, T29 and T31.

— *Amendment 2026-11-11:* `spec` recorded the layout with `NOTICE` and `LICENSE` at the root of the archive by the 2026-11-11 amendment to R85 spec and the card architecture 3.1 spec — the answer to the row «в реестр spec» В49, option (a), by the recommendation; the rollout of `infra` takes it by Д208 of RUN.md of `spec`. The row left table 3 of RUN.md. So it was recorded in R26 at T32. — [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.7.

— *Amendment 2026-11-12:* **the dry run is over, and the release is published: `publish: ${{ github.event_name != 'pull_request' }}` in `.github/workflows/release.yml`** — a run after a green CI on `main` and on the button publishes the release, and a run of a pull request is still a dry run that publishes nothing, as at `canvas` and `skill-map`. The condition of the item "Consequence" is met: the author's step Д54 of RUN.md of `spec` is done — by the GitHub API, read on 2026-11-12, `immutable-releases` is `"enabled":true`, and the ruleset `v-tags` (27046397, since 2026-11-11) has the target `tag`, `refs/tags/v*`, the rules `update`, `deletion` and `non_fast_forward`, and an empty `bypass_actors`. The first release is v0.1.0, by the first run on `main` after the merge of T31: `paths` cannot be compared with a past release, and the shared `release.yml` always puts out a first release. — [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.1; RUN.md, T31.

## The move to `spec` v0.15.0

**R26. `app` stands on `spec` v0.15.0: the submodule `spec/` and the packages `@altnovatrail/spec` and `@altnovatrail/contracts` are at this tag, the part `deployment/` is the file of its release, the rows В43–В49 are closed by the answers of `spec`, and what the tag decided for `app` is laid out across its documents by this same task, T32, with the texts in force at account creation (R27).** The author's answer of 2026-11-11: one task, not two as T13 and T14 were; the rest are the plan's proposals, which the author approved with it (RUN.md, T32, «Решено автором»).
- **What the tag brings `app`** (`git -C spec diff v0.13.0 v0.15.0`, read on 2026-11-11; table 4 and table 5 of RUN.md of `spec`). v0.15.0 took in v0.14.0 too: the end of epic E4 of `spec` and the start of E9:
  - the answers to В43–В49, all by the recommendations of `app`: В43 — row 6.6 of [COORDINATION.md](../spec/COORDINATION.md) is rewritten; В44 — the README of `canvas` will name the consumer's copy of `spec` by R73 spec (Д186 of RUN.md of `spec`), which is the business of `canvas`; В45 — the 2026-11-10 amendment to R78 spec, a script on `@iconify/utils`; В46 — Д177 of RUN.md of `spec` and [the part `deployment/`](../spec/deployment/README.md), section 5; В47 — Д195 of RUN.md of `spec`, the container's name by `--network-alias` by task T115 of `spec`, which is not there yet on v0.15.0; В48 — what `@firebase/app` sends to the sign-in service is recorded in privacy 2 spec, architecture 3.12 spec and the DPIA; В49 — the 2026-11-11 amendment to R85 spec and Д208 of RUN.md of `spec`;
  - the texts in force: `privacy-policy` of version 2 with a translation into Russian, `terms-of-use` and `storage-consent` of version 1 (R138, R141 spec), and the family's texts — `parent-consent`, `teen-consent`, `child-notice` and `teen-notice` (R132, R141 spec); `legal/` — versions 2, with translations (the 2026-11-11 amendment to R130 spec); the operator in the texts is ALT EdTech LLC (R137 spec);
  - the consent contract (R136 spec): `CreateAccount` with the versions of the texts accepted, `SendConsentForm`, `CancelChildConsent`, `GiveOwnConsent`, `WithdrawOwnConsent`, `RestateAge`, `SetWorkStorage` and `AttemptService.ReportProblem` (R123 spec), the profile's consent in `Profile.consent`, and the causes 39–42 — `CAUSE_TOO_MANY_CONSENTS`, `CAUSE_AGE_NEEDED`, `CAUSE_TEXT_NOT_CURRENT` and `CAUSE_LEARNER_DECIDES`;
  - the age of digital consent by country — the part `consent-age/` with the codes of `Country` (R134 spec), the export format — the part `export/` (R135 spec), the laws of the US states — privacy 3.4 spec and R140 spec;
  - the module format is still 0.4, and `RULES.md`, the template and `docs-check` are unchanged; the part `deployment/` is the same version 1, but the release file is another.
- **What changes here:**
  - `spec/` is at v0.15.0, commit 58d3f46; `gh release verify v0.15.0 -R altnovatrail/spec` gives "Release v0.15.0 verified!". `spec_tag` in `justfile` is v0.15.0, and `parts/deployment-1.tar.gz` with its signature is from the release v0.15.0: `release-verify` compares the commit in the archive's header with the tag;
  - `@altnovatrail/contracts` and `@altnovatrail/spec` are 0.15.0 (the 2026-11-11 amendment to R15); `NOTICE` has the line of the contracts 0.15.0;
  - `.devcontainer/`: `diff -r` with the output of `just -f spec/justfile new-repo-draft app typescript 3.1 v0.15.0` gives only the second network `novatrail-api` by R19, as on v0.13.0;
  - the tag in CLAUDE.md, README and RUN.md is v0.15.0; table 3 is without В43–В49, with a note above it on where their answers are;
  - the code: the list of countries is the type `Country` (Д175 of RUN.md of `spec`), the words of the causes 39–42, `GetLegalTexts` by the open transport, as the open views are, and the screen of the texts at account creation (R27);
  - the 2026-11-11 amendments to R03 (В45), R10 (the translation), R15 (the packages), R19 (В46, В47 and the texts) and R25 (В49);
  - what the tag decided for `app` beyond this — the rows Д144, Д166, Д168, Д175, Д180, Д183, Д190, Д195, Д201 and Д204 in table 4 of RUN.md of `spec` and R132–R141 spec — is laid out by sections 1, 3.2, 3.6, 3.8 and 3.10 in [architecture.md](architecture.md) and by the rows E3 and E4 of table 2 of RUN.md.

— *Why now:* the row Д190 of RUN.md of `spec`: while `app` sends no versions of the texts, `api` at the tag with T110 refuses `CreateAccount`, and the first sign-in of the first app breaks; the rows Д144, Д166, Д168, Д175, Д180, Д183, Д195, Д201 and Д204 wait for the same move, and the answers to В43–В49 close all the open rows «в реестр spec» of `app` (RULES.md, Across repositories). The release v0.15.0 is the latest (`gh release list -R altnovatrail/spec`, 2026-11-11).

— *Rejected:*
- the intermediate v0.14.0: v0.15.0 came out half a day after it and carries the translation of the policy, the operator and the family's texts in force, and the next move would have to be made at once;
- two tasks, as T13 and T14 — the author's answer: less is decided for `app` than on v0.13.0, and the screens of the consent, the teenager and the export stay for the epics E3 and E4;
- the screen of the texts in E3, with the legal pages: then T30 would go only with an `api` at a tag without T110 (the author's answer).

— *Consequence:* T32 in RUN.md; R27; the 2026-11-11 amendments to R03, R10, R15, R19 and R25. — CLAUDE.md; README.md; RUN.md, T32 and tables 1–3; `justfile`; `parts/`; `NOTICE`; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 1, 3.1, 3.2, 3.6, 3.7, 3.8, 3.10 and 4.

**R27. With no account, `app` shows on the invite code screen the texts `privacy-policy` and `terms-of-use` that `GetLegalTexts` gives in force, and asks for the tick "I accept"; `CreateAccount` names the versions of those shown, and on `CAUSE_TEXT_NOT_CURRENT` the screen reads the texts anew and asks to accept again. The body of a text is Markdown, which the lexer of `marked` 18.1.3 reads, and code of its own builds the elements, with no string of HTML. `unimplemented` from an `api` without `LegalService` is "no text in force".** The author's answer of 2026-11-11: the screen in the move itself; the rest are the plan's proposals, which the author approved with it (RUN.md, T32, «Решено автором»). The design is in [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.2, 3.7 and 3.8, and the sources: section 4.
- **The screen.** Under the code and "I am 18", the heading "Privacy Policy and Terms of Use", each text unfolding in place, without leaving the page and without a link outside, and the tick "I accept the Privacy Policy and the Terms of Use". Without the tick, a field error and the focus on it, as for "I am 18". While the texts are not read, the button "Create account" is off; a failure of the reading is its words and "Try again". While the texts are read anew, the previous ones stay on the screen, so the tick loses neither its place nor the focus.
- **The text.** In the unfolding: the first-level heading of the body, `lang="en"`; the gist, with the `lang` of the field `summary_language`; on a screen not in English, a line that the text is in English and the English one is in force; the translation, if the version carries one, with its own `lang` and the note "for reading only"; and the English body, `lang="en"`. The body's headings go below the page's level: the first is of the third level.
- **The versions.** `CreateAccount` names `{id, version}` of each text shown; with no texts in force it names none, as the contract asks. The tick holds to the versions at which it was put: if others are shown — for example, after a change of language or a repeated reading —, it is removed. `CAUSE_TEXT_NOT_CURRENT` removes the tick and reads the texts anew, and when no text was on the screen, says so in a line under the button ([the contracts text](../spec/contracts/README.md), section 15, "The versions of the texts").
- **The transport.** `GetLegalTexts` is a Connect GET without a token, without a deadline and without headers, as the package's open views are, with the screen's language in `interface_language`, but past the HTTP cache, `cache: "no-cache"`: the consent names the version shown, and a version from the cache after a new one came into force would get a refusal, and the retry the same one again.
- **`unimplemented`.** `LegalService` came into the contract with v0.13.0, and `api` on `main` stands on v0.12.0: it has no texts in force, and `CreateAccount` does not check them. So for `GetLegalTexts`, `unimplemented` is an empty list, without an offer to reload, and for the other methods it is still the offer (R18). A 404 of a proxy that does not route the path of `LegalService` comes out the same: Connect reads it as `unimplemented`. But an `api` with texts in force refuses `CreateAccount` without their versions, and the screen says so, so without the texts no account is created.
- **Markdown.** The part `legal/` writes CommonMark with GitHub tables, without HTML, pictures and links ([the part `legal/`](../spec/legal/README.md), section 4). The lexer of `marked` gives tokens, and `src/legal/Markdown.tsx` builds React elements from them; HTML, a link and a picture come out as their own text. Named character references are those of the markup and the typography of a legal text, from `&amp;` to quotation marks and dashes, and `marked` itself expands the numeric ones; in inline code they stay as written, as CommonMark asks. The heading in the unfolding is the words of the heading without markup.

— *Why:* without the versions, `api` at the tag with T110 refuses `CreateAccount` (Д190 of RUN.md of `spec`), and the version that the consent names must be the one the person saw (R131 spec). Elements without a string of HTML give the text neither a script nor a link outside, and the CSP need not allow inline markup (section 3.7).

— *Rejected:*
- `mdast-util-from-markdown` with the extension of GitHub tables — 12 dependencies against none for `marked`, and the tree is the same;
- HTML from `marked` and `innerHTML` — a string of markup on the page, which the part `legal/` does not write and which would have to be sanitized;
- a separate page of the text by a route — leaving the form where the code and the tick "I am 18" are entered;
- taking `unimplemented` for an outdated build, as for the other methods: the invite screen on `api` v0.12.0 would offer a reload, which changes nothing.

— *Consequence:* `src/legal/` — `texts.ts`, `body.ts`, `Markdown.tsx` and `LegalText.tsx`; `InviteScreen` and `Session.createAccount`; `effects` in `src/api/interceptors.ts`; the keys `account.texts*` and `legal.*` in the dictionaries. The link "privacy" on the start screen and in the settings, and a new version of the policy before it applies — E3 (Д204 of RUN.md of `spec`). — [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.2, 3.7, 3.8 and 4; RUN.md, T32.

**R28. `app` stands on `spec` v0.15.1: the submodule `spec/`, the packages `@altnovatrail/spec` and `@altnovatrail/contracts` and the part `deployment/` are at this tag, and `.claude/settings.json` and `.devcontainer/` follow the tag's template: one task is one session, and the hook `UserPromptSubmit` holds it, compaction at 400 thousand tokens, the claude.ai connectors off, Go 1.27.4. The rest of what R143 spec assigns to the repositories — the translation of `docs/`, a thin CLAUDE.md, the bodies of done tasks to the archive at once — `app` takes by task T123 of `spec`, not by this move.** The author's answer of 2026-11-11: move to the latest tag of `spec`; the rest is the executor's proposal (RUN.md, T33).
- **What the tag brings `app`** (`git -C spec diff v0.15.0 v0.15.1`, read on 2026-11-11; [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 4):
  - RULES.md, How we work: "One task is one session (R143 spec)" — the planning and the execution of a task run in one session, and the next task in a new one or after `/clear`; the hook `UserPromptSubmit` from `spec/tools/session/` stops a phrase that starts another task in the same session;
  - the template: `.claude/settings.json` — `autoCompactWindow` 400000, `disableClaudeAiConnectors` and this hook; the line about one session in «Как пользоваться» of RUN.md; Go 1.27.4 in place of 1.27.3 in `.devcontainer/Dockerfile` — 1.27.3 has the vulnerability GO-2026-6859 of the standard library (the 2026-11-11 amendment to R64 spec). `app` has no Go code of its own, but the version of Go is one for all the repositories and comes from the stage `toolchain`;
  - the contract: the field `teaching_modules` of `AnchorSkillState` — the catalog's modules that teach the anchor skill and are not connected to the profile (the 2026-11-11 amendment to R99 spec). `app` must show them when the anchor is "Unknown", with the name from the open view of the manifest and connecting by `ConnectModule` (Д211 of RUN.md of `spec`); this is the skill map screen, E3. The field is only added, and the build of `app` without the map skips it (R83 spec);
  - the module format is still 0.4; the parts are of the same versions; `docs-check` and the rule "Language" of RULES.md are unchanged: `docs/` are still in Russian.
- **What changes here:**
  - `spec/` is at v0.15.1, commit 6a4ba48; `gh release verify v0.15.1 -R altnovatrail/spec` gives "Release v0.15.1 verified!"; `spec_tag` in `justfile` is v0.15.1, and `parts/deployment-1.tar.gz` with its signature is from the release v0.15.1: the contents are the same, but `release-verify` compares the commit in the archive's header with the tag;
  - `@altnovatrail/contracts` and `@altnovatrail/spec` are 0.15.1 (the second 2026-11-11 amendment to R15); `NOTICE` has the line of the contracts 0.15.1;
  - `.devcontainer/`: `diff -r` with the output of `just -f spec/justfile new-repo-draft app typescript 3.1 v0.15.1` gives only the second network `novatrail-api` by R19; `.claude/settings.json` is the same as in the recipe's output;
  - RUN.md: the line about one session in «Как пользоваться», as in the template; the tag in CLAUDE.md, README and RUN.md is v0.15.1;
  - [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.10: `teaching_modules` in the row of the skill map, and the row E3 of table 2 of RUN.md.

— *Why now:* v0.15.1 is the latest release (`gh release list -R altnovatrail/spec`, 2026-11-11). A container on 1.27.3 would carry a vulnerable Go, and without the hook a session that leads one task would start the next one too, against the RULES.md of this same tag.

— *Rejected:*
- waiting for the tag with T122 and T123 of `spec`: Go and the hook are needed now, and the next move will be a task of its own anyway;
- the translation of `docs/` and the rest by R143 spec already in this move: RULES.md on v0.15.1 still asks for `docs/` in Russian, and the tag has no checks of the translation, no rules of RUN.md about the archive and no thin CLAUDE.md — T132 and T136–T139 of `spec` give them, and T123 of `spec` the task for `app`;
- showing `teaching_modules` in this move: there is no skill map in E2, and the detailing of `app` and `skill-map` decides the showing (Д211 of RUN.md of `spec`).

— *Consequence:* T33 in RUN.md; the second 2026-11-11 amendment to R15; Rebuild Container after the merge, for Go 1.27.4. — CLAUDE.md; README.md; RUN.md, T33, «Как пользоваться» and tables 1 and 2; `justfile`; `parts/`; `NOTICE`; `.claude/settings.json`; `.devcontainer/Dockerfile`; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.1, 3.10 and 4.

— *Amendment 2026-11-12:* the rest of what R143 spec assigns to the repositories `app` takes not by a separate task by the text of T123 of `spec`, but by the move to v0.16.3, T53 — the author's decision of 2026-11-12: the restructuring of the documents in it (R39), and the translation of `docs/` by the tasks T54 and T55. — RUN.md, T53.

## Section for adults

**R29.
