  - a fraction with a denominator from 2 to 12 is read by the name of its parts, otherwise "a over b" and «a делённое на b»;
  - a language without a table sounds with its signs as they are.

  The screen shows the text as it is.
- **The page "This device"**, the section "Voicing" after the profiles:
  - the languages are the build's `en` and `ru` and the explanation languages of the modules whose manifests the cache holds;
  - each language has a group of radio buttons: the candidates, with the voice taken now marked; a choice is written at once, without "Save";
  - the sample is in English or in Russian, in the voice's language: only for the build's languages, and on Android and Linux only after the permission;
  - a language without a voice has, in words, how to add a voice in the system;
  - a browser without Web Speech API has a line that it does not read aloud.

— *Why:*
- 0.75 is markedly slower, but not twice as slow: "half as fast", 0.5, is the edge, not the middle, and without a check on devices there is no reason to take the edge;
- `userAgentData` first: the specification names its values (section 4), while the user agent string names no values of its own and is read by substrings. The string is a fallback for a browser without `userAgentData`;
- the button at the top of the screen: it is one, and it is seen in both modes before the first words, while the tutor's panel appears and disappears;
- the line is a description, not a status: it stands from the start, and a status at each voicing button would be read on every screen together with the text;
- the cache's languages, not those of the connected modules: only `api` knows the connected modules, profile by profile, and the device page speaks of what is on this device. A module the cache does not hold yet will appear in the list once it is opened;
- the sample only after the permission: before it, the sample's text could go to the network just as the tutor's words could;
- the choice of a voice without "Save": it is a setting of the device, it sends nothing and changes no context (3.2.2, section 3.9);
- a hidden working does not sound with its boxes: the demo is hidden whole, its open parts stay open but are not seen (R42 canvas).

— *Rejected:*
- "slow" as 0.5, or a number of its own for each engine before T51: without a check any number is a guess, and one number is simpler to correct;
- the platform by features of the API: no feature says whether the system synthesizes on the device;
- the learner's button on the tutor's panel — the argument above;
- the line "no voice" as a status, as the row 4.1.3 of section 3.9 recorded it before T45 — the argument above;
- the languages of each profile's connected modules through `ListConnectedModules` — a request per profile for a page that speaks of the device;
- SSML instead of the table: Web Speech API allows it, but section 4 did not check how the engines read it, and the table is plain text, the same for all;
- "Read aloud" at the task's solution — section 3.5 does not name it; the word «Послушать» for the sample — it is close to «прослушать», and concepts 8 spec keeps that word for the text canvas.

— *Does not decide:*
- the number of the "slow" speed and the check of the code on devices — Д5, T51;
- how the engines read signs that are not in the table of Д6 — `+`, the `:` of division "300 : 100" beside the time "4:50", `%` and the decimal point — Д18, T51;
- the texts of modules in `ru`: the first slice has none, and the table for `ru` is checked with synthetic texts;
- the "listen" of the text canvas — with it, through the same synthesis (R36 spec);
- the persona's voice — О-13.

— *Consequence:*
- `src/voice/`:
  - `voices.ts` — the voice, the platform and the speed;
  - `spoken.ts` — the table of Д6 and the sentences;
  - `texts.ts` — what the button reads;
  - `speech.ts` and `browser.ts` — the synthesis;
  - `VoicingContext.tsx` — the voicing of the learner's screens;
  - `ReadAloud.tsx`, `VoicingToggle.tsx` and `DeviceVoices.tsx`;
- `src/storage/voicing.ts` — the device's record; `src/test/speech.ts` — the synthesis of the tests;
- voicing in `src/task/Task.tsx`, the buttons in `TaskScreen.tsx`, `TutorWords.tsx`, `Hint.tsx`, `StepsPanel.tsx`, `src/lesson/Explanation.tsx` and `Example.tsx`;
- `VoicingProvider` in `src/app/App.tsx`, the learner's `onSaved` and `onFailed` in `src/account/AccountGate.tsx`, `useOptionalPackages` in `src/packages/PackagesContext.tsx`;
- the section "Voicing" in `src/adults/DevicePage.tsx`, a new line in `ProfilePage.tsx`;
- the icon `read-aloud` in `scripts/icons.ts`, the keys `voice.*` in the dictionaries, `.nt-read-aloud` in `theme.css`.

— [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.1, 3.2, 3.5, 3.8, 3.9 and 4; RUN.md, T45, Д5 and Д18.

— *Amendment 2026-11-12:* **what sounds by itself now includes the line of the quota or of the platform AI limit: under the rung's fallback text, after the words, and after a correct answer without words, after the verdict, in the screen's language.** The executor decided it during T49, and the author reviews it (R37).

## Platform AI quota on the screens

**R37. When the platform AI quota comes back, `app` writes it by the device's calendar: "today at {time}", "tomorrow at {time}", and later "on {date} at {time}". The block "Tutor" names the quota and the platform AI limit both under the rung's fallback text and in the place of the correct answer words, while an AI failure and "not admitted" give no line there. The contract v0.15.1 gives the quota share neither to the adult nor to the learner, and `app` has none until a move to a tag of `spec` gives it.** The executor's decision of 2026-11-12 in the planning of T49: the author was unavailable, and by the author's one-time instruction of 2026-11-12 the tasks ran in a row without the author; the author reviews the decision (RUN.md, T49, «Решено при планировании»). The design is in [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.3, 3.8 and 3.10; the normative text is [the contracts' text](../spec/contracts/README.md), section 15, and the comments of `Cause`, `Refusal`, `Outcome` and `EngineChoice` in `cause.proto`.
- **What has been there since E2** (R18) and does not change:
  - the outcome of "Check" is a line at the button, and the answer slots the AI did not read are "Not checked";
  - the refusals `CAUSE_QUOTA` at the start of a task — "What's next", "Try it again" and a lesson's segment — and at "Explain differently" are a line, and the right to "differently" stays;
  - "nothing to give" is "There is nothing more to offer for now" with the choice's outcome;
  - the clock offset goes in `SubmitWork` with handwriting, `GetRungWords`, `GetCorrectAnswerWords` and `ExplainDifferently` (`aiFields`), as the contract requires: "with a request on platform AI alone". `SubmitEssay` comes with T47, and `BeginAttempt` and `ChooseNext` have no such field.
- **When it comes back.** The day `next_family_day` is compared with today by the device's calendar, in its time zone:
  - the same day is "today at {time}". So is a time of today that the device's clock has already passed: the clock runs fast against `api`'s clock;
  - the next one is "tomorrow at {time}";
  - later is "on {date} at {time}", the day and the month in words, without the year. This happens after a change of the offset: the first family day by the new one lasts up to 44 hours (R56 api). A day before today, for a clock off by more than a day, is also written as a date: "today" about a day gone by would be untrue.

  The time is `timeStyle: "short"`, as before. There are three strings, each a whole phrase of the dictionary, not a concatenation.
- **Under the rung's fallback text** is the quota line or the neutral phrase of the platform AI limit, in the block "Tutor", under the words. Voicing says it after the words, in the screen's language.
- **One line, not two.** If the check has already said it — as a line at "Check" — the words of the same check do not repeat it. A refusal of "Explain differently" with the same text is not repeated under the line, and the right to "differently" stays, and the button too: with the start of the next family day it gives words again. When the model's words come, the line goes.
- **After a correct answer without words** is the block "Tutor" with the same line, above the solution; voicing says it after the verdict. The line of the earlier rung's words goes with the correct answer.
- **An AI failure and "not admitted" give no line at the words** — neither under the fallback text nor after a correct answer. This is `closesPlatformAI` in `src/api/outcome.ts`.
- **Quota share.** The contract v0.15.1 has no request of the share: `app` gets no tariff, no caps and no counters. The export `ExportAccount` carries the counters in dollars, but without the caps. The adult's request is the epic E4 of `spec`, and `app` takes it by a move to the tag (Д19). The share for the learner that concepts 14 spec speaks of was not left by R98 spec to the epic E4 — В60. Until the answer, the section for adults has no page and no line of the share.

— *Why:*
- "tomorrow": «Текст — у `app`: числа он не называет, а «завтра» обещать может» (architecture 3.6 api). A time alone, without a day, on a family day of up to 44 hours does not say which day is meant. So the run of T52 `api` of 2026-11-12 and the row `api` В60;
- the device's calendar: the offset by which `api` counts the family day is sent by this same device, and `Intl` writes the time in its zone. A family day after a change of the offset does not end at midnight, and only the day on the device's clock is the one the learner sees;
- the line under the fallback text: in the run of T52 `api` the fallback text came without it, and the learner would not learn why the tutor speaks in general words and why the next check is "Not checked" (the row `api` В60);
- the quota and the limit are what closes the platform AI for longer than one call: until the next family day or until the operator's command (R59 api). An AI failure is a matter of one call, and "not admitted" is a lasting state of the deployment. `api` does not admit the correct answer words now (Д25 `api`), and the line "The tutor isn't available for this profile" would stand at every correct answer, telling nothing.

— *Rejected:*
- a date always, "on 13 November at 00:00": longer for a child of 6, and "tomorrow" is right almost always;
- "in N hours": the line stays on the screen, and the number ages with every minute;
- a line at each outcome of the words, as at "Check" — the argument above about "not admitted";
- the day by the offset from the request, not by the device's zone: it is the same number of the same device, and `Intl` writes the time by the zone too;
- a page of the share of its own now: without the request it has nothing to show.

— *Does not decide:*
- a line shown before midnight stays until the next response. A tablet left overnight would say "tomorrow" in the morning about today, until the next check brings a new response;
- the quota share — Д19 and В60;
- a run with `api` on the tariff `probe` — until the answers to В52 and В58 (RUN.md, T49, «Открыто»).

— *Consequence:* `calendarDays` and the quota words in `src/api/words.ts`, and an optional `now` for `outcomeText` and `failureText`; `closesPlatformAI` in `src/api/outcome.ts`; the line at the rung's words and after a correct answer in `src/task/Task.tsx`, the block "Tutor" in the task's outcome in `TaskScreen.tsx`; the keys `api.quota`, `api.quota-today` and `api.quota-tomorrow` in the dictionaries. — [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.3, 3.8 and 3.10; RUN.md, T49, Д19 and В60.

— *Amendment 2026-11-12:* `app` has the quota share — `GetQuota` of the contract v0.16.3 (R38): for the learner, a line under the heading "My subjects"; for the adult, the section "Tutor time" on the profile page; and the section writes the day when the quota comes back by the same rules, through `dayText` (R40). "Quota share" and "Does not decide" about it no longer hold: Д19 is closed, and В60 is closed by the 2026-11-12 amendment to R98 spec.

Of the tasks of `spec`, only T143 is done in the tag; T141 and T144–T147 are open, and `app` takes them by a move to their tag;

## The structure of the documents by R143 spec

**R39. `app`'s documents are laid out by R143 spec, as the shared text of T123 `spec` requires: table 4 is in `DEBT.md`; a done task's body goes to its epic's archive at once, and a sentence with the gist stays in RUN.md; external facts are records of `docs/sources.md`, the results of measurements are in `docs/measurements.md`, and in `docs/architecture.md`, section 4 is a pointer; CLAUDE.md follows the template of v0.16.3, and the checks of the code are the rule `.claude/rules/code.md` by paths. The translation of `docs/` into English is two tasks, T54 and T55, and `docs-budgets.json` comes with the last of them.** The author's decision of 2026-11-12 in the planning of T53 — row 7.4 of COORDINATION.md of `spec` by this task, in parts, without the pilot 7.1; the cut into parts and the rest are the executor's proposals (RUN.md, T53).
- **`DEBT.md`** is table 4 word for word, with its opening and its next number, by the template; in RUN.md, a pointer under the same heading stands in its place. The references «Д… в таблице 4 RUN.md» in the log, the archives and the documents before T53 are not edited: it is the same register, as T132 `spec` decided.
- **The archive at once.** The bodies of T35–T45 and T49 go to the new archive [plan/E3.md](plan/E3.md), word for word, with their relative links corrected; in RUN.md, their sentences with the gist stand before the bodies of the tasks of E3 still under way. A done task outside the epic goes to the archive's section «Задачи вне эпика, сделанные за его время» (the 2026-11-12 amendment to R13). RUN.md went 287 → 63 KB.
- **Sources and measurements** follow the example of T133 `spec`: a record is a numbered subsection of its topic, a citation is `sources 3.2`; the results of measurements — T25 with the mouse and the one-time run of T34 in WebKit and Firefox — are in `docs/measurements.md`; section 4 stays a pointer with the table "reading — record", because the log and the archives cite "section 4" and are not rewritten. The new files are in Russian, as all of `docs/` is until the translation: T54 translates them.
- **CLAUDE.md** — "Your role", "Documents" and "Environment" follow the template of v0.16.3: `docs/sources.md`, `docs/measurements.md` and `DEBT.md` in the list of documents, `docs/` in English, and in Russian until T54 and T55 have translated them. `app` has one part with checks of its own — the code: its checks are the rule `.claude/rules/code.md` with the paths of the code's files, and CLAUDE.md names it in one line (R143 spec).
- **The translation** follows the [order](../spec/tools/translation/README.md) of `spec`: T54 — `docs/architecture.md`, `docs/sources.md` and `docs/measurements.md`, ≈ 620 KB of Russian; T55 — the log, ≈ 480 KB, and with it `docs-budgets.json` and the measurement "after". `spec` translated ≈ 460–580 KB per task (T136–T139 `spec`).
- **The measurement.** "Before" is the row `-workspaces-app` in measurements 1.9 spec; "after" is `just -f spec/justfile tokens-report --since 2026-11-12T20:23 --row` over the sessions of T53–T55, in «Сделано» of T55.

— *Why:* R143 spec — RUN.md, the debt and the bodies of done tasks are read by every session, though it needs only the live rows; the table of differences of T123 `spec` names for `app` a RUN.md of 102 KB, of which table 4 is 71 KB, and 9 sections of `docs/architecture.md` over 24 KB. The translation is separate because ≈ 1.1 MB cannot be checked in one task (rule 11).

— *Rejected:*
- the translation in T53 — more than one check, and the blind reading of 1.1 MB takes several subagents (rule 11);
- three or four translation tasks — `spec` translated more per task, and the log is read by entries and in pieces of 15–30 KB;
- `docs-budgets.json` now — the limits would have to be set on the Russian text, which the translation will shrink: step 7 of the text of T123 `spec` raises only what stays over after the translation;
- no rule for the code — Environment by the template names no parts, but the checks of the code would then live only in table 1 of RUN.md, which the session reads whole, while a rule loads when the session touches the code.

— *Consequence:* `DEBT.md`; [plan/E3.md](plan/E3.md); `docs/sources.md`; `docs/measurements.md`; `.claude/rules/code.md`; the 2026-11-12 amendment to R13; CLAUDE.md; RUN.md — «Как пользоваться», rules 2, 6, 9 and 10, «Как устроен план: эпики», table 1, T53–T55; [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 4 and the links to the records.

## Names in the progress and the quota share

**R40. The adult's progress names a lesson by its name, and a solved attempt also by the skills of its task, by their names. `app` takes the platform AI quota share by `GetQuota`: for the learner, as a line under the heading "My subjects", the share of the learner's own profile, and when the cap is reached, the words of the quota refusal (R37); for the adult, as the section "Tutor time" at the bottom of the profile page: the shares of the profile and of the account, that the cap is reached, and when the next family day begins. From an `api` without `GetQuota`, neither the line nor the section.** The author's decision of 2026-11-12 in the planning of T53 — the code for the new contract in T53 at once, though `api` does not give it yet; the design is the executor's proposals (RUN.md, T53). The contract is v0.16.3 (R38): the 2026-11-12 amendments to R98 and R99 spec, Д228 and Д229 `spec`, [the contracts' text](../spec/contracts/README.md), section 15.
- **Names in the progress** (R35):
  - "Lesson" is `lesson_title`, with the `lang` of the module's explanation language. An empty name — `api` no longer reads the package of the attempt's version — gives the local ID, as before R40;
  - "Skills" comes after "Lesson", before "Version": `skill_titles` as a list by `Intl.ListFormat` of the screen's language, `conjunction` — "A, B and C" —, each name in a `<span lang>` of the module's explanation language, and the commas and the "and" in the screen's language. Only for a solved attempt — closed with `CLOSE_REASON_CORRECT`, `CLOSE_REASON_SOLUTION_SHOWN` or `CLOSE_REASON_DONE` (`skillTitlesOf` in `src/adults/progress.ts`): the skills of a task are closed while it can be begun again, because their names hint at the solution ([the format text](../spec/format/README.md), section 14), and the screen does not show the names for another attempt if `api` sends them;
  - the language of the names comes from the manifest of the module's current version or from the cache, as for the texts of a trap (R35), though the contract takes the names from the package of the attempt's version: the `lang` is wrong only if the module changed its explanation language between versions.
- **Reading the share** is `readQuota` in `src/account/quota.ts`: `GetQuota` with `ACTION_LEARNING` or `ACTION_ADULT`. `unimplemented` from an `api` on a tag older than v0.16.3 is not a share and offers no reload: `GetQuota` is in `MAY_BE_ABSENT` of `src/api/interceptors.ts`, as `GetLegalTexts` is (R27). A response without `share` is not a share either. The percentages are `Intl.NumberFormat` with `style: "percent"` of the screen's language: "40 %", "40%".
- **For the learner** — a line `<p>` under the heading "My subjects", above "Continue": "Tutor time today: {percent} used". The share is read when the screen opens, and again on the lists' "Try again": it may not have come with them. With `cap_reached` — the words of the quota refusal of R37, "Today's tutor time is used up. It comes back …", and without `next_family_day` — the neutral phrase, as for the refusal: the contract says that `cap_reached` for a learning action means the same as the refusal `CAUSE_QUOTA`. Without a percentage — the tariff has no caps, the platform AI is closed — there is no line; on a failure there is none either, and the lists stay as they are. The line does not show the account's share, even if it came. A child's profile, which comes with E4, gets the same line.
- **For the adult** — the section "Tutor time" on the profile page, the last one, under "Progress" (`src/adults/QuotaSection.tsx`): a heading `h2`, a line saying what it is — "How much of today's tutor time — the platform's AI, which checks work and words the tutor's replies — this profile and the whole account have used" — and the lines in order:
  - "This profile: {percent} used" and "The whole account: {percent} used";
  - with `cap_reached` — "Today's tutor time is used up: until it renews, the platform's AI checks no work of this profile";
  - "Tutor time renews today at {time}", "… tomorrow at {time}" or "… on {date} at {time}" — by the device's calendar, by the rules of R37: `dayText` in `src/api/words.ts` is what R37 writes the quota refusal with, with the section's three phrases;
  - without both percentages — the one line "The platform's AI has no tutor time on this account now: it is closed to it".

  While the request is under way, and from an `api` without `GetQuota`, there is no section: an empty heading would say nothing. A failure gives its words and "Try again" under the heading, as on the first page of the progress (R35).

— *Why:*
- the name of the lesson and of the skills tells the adult what the child worked on, and a local ID does not; R35 left the names to В57, and В57 is closed by the 2026-11-12 amendment to R99 spec;
- the learner's line is where the learner begins work: so the contract names the place of a learning action, and the learner sees the share before going to the tutor. A plain line, not a live region: no action of the learner called it;
- the section is on the profile page, not on the section's first page as R29 proposed: `GetQuota` asks for a profile, and the account's share stands beside the profile's share, because the account's cap is shared by all the profiles;
- the words of R37 when the cap is reached: one state — one phrase on all screens;
- without a percentage the learner sees no line: "closed" on the first screen gives a child nothing, and the call that meets the closed AI will say its own.

— *Rejected:*
- waiting for the move of `api` to v0.16.3, row 6.13 of COORDINATION.md of `spec` — the executor's recommendation; the author decided to do it at once (R38);
- the share on the section's first page or on a page of its own (R29): the request is a profile's, and the page would have three lines;
- the list of skills in the style `unit`: in Russian, `Intl.ListFormat` with it puts only spaces, without commas;
- the account's share for the learner if `api` sends it: "A child never sees what the family spends" ([the contracts' text](../spec/contracts/README.md), section 15).

— *Does not decide:* the check with a real `api` — until its move to the tag, row 6.13, and T56 (Д20 in `DEBT.md`); a line telling the child that the adult sees their progress — E4, as in R35.

— *Consequence:* `src/account/quota.ts`; `src/adults/QuotaSection.tsx` and the section in `src/adults/ProfilePage.tsx`; the line in `src/subjects/MySubjects.tsx`; `skillTitlesOf` in `src/adults/progress.ts`, and "Lesson" and "Skills" in `src/adults/ProgressPage.tsx`; `dayText` in `src/api/words.ts`; `listFormat` and `percentText` in `src/i18n/intl.ts`; `GetQuota` in `MAY_BE_ABSENT` of `src/api/interceptors.ts`; the keys `quota.*` and `progress.skills` in the dictionaries; tests. The 2026-11-12 amendments to R29, R35 and R37. Д19 is closed. — [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.8 and 3.10; DEBT.md, Д20; RUN.md, T53.

## RUN.md: only what a session acts on

**R41. RUN.md has no section «Задачи вне эпика», and what in the current epic's part has stopped being live goes to the epic's archive before the epic ends. A task outside an epic has its body in the epic's part and its summary sentence, marked «вне эпика», in the epic's list of done tasks; when there is no epic, its body stands at the end of the file. The summary sentences of done tasks are short: the gist and the decision, the detail is in the body in the archive.** The author's word of 2026-11-13, after T56 turned out to wait for `spec` and `api`: to remove the section, which no longer held a task, and to clean RUN.md of what the executor does not need, for the tokens of every session that reads it whole (R143 spec).
- **Moved to [plan/E3.md](plan/E3.md)**, word for word, with the links corrected, into the sections «Закрытые строки таблицы 3» and «Из описания эпика в RUN.md»: the history of the closed rows В50–В60, the reconnaissance of the neighbours in the planning of E3 on 2026-11-12, the decision on В51 that T38 carried out, the points of «Почему такой порядок» about done tasks, the lines about how the epic was cut into tasks, and the summary sentences of T35–T55 as they were before they were shortened. A task that relies on the reconnaissance checks the neighbours again on the day of its planning.
- **A difference from the template.** The template's RUN.md of `spec` v0.16.3 and R145 spec name the section «Задачи вне эпика»; in `app`, a task of analysis by the threshold of R145 spec goes where any task outside an epic goes. A move to a tag of `spec` that brings a new template takes this difference into account.

— *Why:* every session reads RUN.md whole (R143 spec): a section without a task, the history of answered rows and a snapshot of the neighbours that newer tags have overtaken cost tokens in each of them and help none. The archive is not read whole, so what moved there costs nothing until a task needs it.

— *Rejected:*
- deleting the moved text — the archive must keep the epic's description word for word (RUN.md, «Как устроен план: эпики»), and what moved early is part of it;
- shortening the rules of RUN.md — they are the template's, and a difference in them makes every move to a new tag harder; the bodies of the open tasks — each is planned and rewritten in its own session.

— *Consequence:* RUN.md — rule 9, «Как устроен план: эпики», the opening, tables 1–3 and the part of E3; [plan/E3.md](plan/E3.md); the 2026-11-13 amendment to R13.

— *Amendment 2026-11-14:* the template of `spec` v0.17.1 names where a done task outside an epic goes when there is no epic: to the archive of the last done epic, into its section «Задачи вне эпика, сделанные после него» (the 2026-11-13 amendment to R19 spec). `app` took its sentence into rule 9 with R43, in the words of this entry: the summary sentence goes to the end of RUN.md, where the body stood, and the sentences of such tasks leave with the archive of the next epic. — RUN.md, rule 9 and «Как устроен план: эпики».

**R42. The deployment of `app` is checked by `app` itself, by the recipe `just site-check <origin> <version>`: the release's archive, accepted by `release-verify`, against what the site serves to anyone — every file of the archive byte for byte, `/deployment.json` as the code of `app` reads it, the headers section 3.7 of [architecture.md](architecture.md) asks of the hosting, and the page in Chromium under the site's own header up to the sign-in window, which it closes before anyone signs in. The sign-in with an account, the invite code and a task on the deployment are not walked again: T24 `infra` walked them. The platform's domain is `novatrail.web.app`.** The author's answers of 2026-11-13 in the planning of T50, the first by the recommendation and the second not by it: T24 `infra` rolled out `app` v0.1.0 with `api` v0.1.22 on 2026-11-12, and the author signed in there with Google and made the account by a code of `api invite`. The newest release of `app` is v0.1.3, on `spec` v0.16.3, and its pair is `api` v0.1.29. To raise the list is E6 of `infra`, and a session of `app` changes no other repository (RULES.md, Across repositories).
- **What the rollout of `infra` checks and what `app` checks.** The rollout holds the archive to the list's digest and to its layout, `/deployment.json` to the schema and to `api_origin`, and after the release of the site the page at `/` and at a path of the SPA, one file of `assets/`, a 404 under `/assets/`, `/deployment.json`, `/NOTICE` and `/LICENSE`, with their headers (`tools/deploy/app.mjs` of `infra`, `main` on 2026-11-13). `site-check` adds:
  - every file of the archive, `csp.json` and `deployment.schema.json` included;
  - the file read by the code of `app` itself, `loadDeployment`;
  - the CSP header built by `csp.json` of the release, by `scripts/csp.ts`;
  - what only a browser shows: no violation of the CSP under the real header with Google's real script and iframe, only the origin of `app` asked before the tap of "Sign in with Google", after it no host beyond those of section 3.7, the window on `https://<authDomain>/__/auth/handler` and the hidden iframe of `authDomain` in the page, and the site's host among the authorized domains of the project, by the rule of the SDK: a name also within a domain, an IP address only exactly.

  It reads the site as anyone does and signs nobody in, so it needs neither the author nor an account (R146 spec). The same recipe checks a self-hosted copy: its operator names the copy's origin.
- **The domain.** `novatrail.web.app`, which the rollout of `infra` sets (R06 infra); an own domain waits for О-16. That it is among the authorized domains of the Firebase project, the recipe sees in the answer of `identitytoolkit.googleapis.com`.
- **The archive** is taken from the release's public address with its signature, by `curl`, not by `gh`: so the recipe needs no token, and a copy's operator can run it too.

— *Why:* the rollout's check is the rollout's: it does not open the page in a browser, and a self-hosted copy has no rollout of `infra` at all, while section 3.7 is the design of `app`. A recipe and not a one-off script, by the author's word, so that it runs again after each rollout — E6 `infra` and T51.

— *Rejected:*
- putting T50 off until E6 `infra` rolls out v0.1.3: E6 itself waits for T50, and the two would wait for each other;
- a one-off script — the recommendation of the planning: no code to keep, but nothing to run again after the next rollout;
- walking the sign-in, the invite code and a task again by hand — R146 spec: the author walks nothing by hand, and on the deployment the sign-in is the author's Google account.

— *Consequence:* `justfile`, `site-check`; `scripts/site.ts` and its test; `scripts/google-stub.ts`, Google's stand-ins out of `scripts/page.test.ts`; [architecture.md](architecture.md), sections 3.1 and 3.7; DEBT.md — Д8 and Д9; RUN.md — T50 and the row «в реестр spec» В61.

— *Amendment 2026-11-14:* the row «в реестр spec» В61 is closed: the 2026-11-13 amendment to R76 spec gives the site the pair `app` v0.1.3 with `api` v0.1.35 or newer, by a task of `infra` outside an epic, row 6.16 of [COORDINATION.md](../spec/COORDINATION.md) (R43). The run after it, `just site-check https://novatrail.web.app v0.1.3`, was T51's and is now T57's; if `infra` has not rolled the pair out by the end of T57, it stays open with row 6.16. — RUN.md, T57.

## The move to `spec` v0.17.1

**R43. `app` stands on `spec` v0.17.1: the submodule `spec/`, the packages `@altnovatrail/spec` and `@altnovatrail/contracts` with `@bufbuild/protobuf` 2.17.0, and the part `deployment/` are at this tag, and `.devcontainer/`, CLAUDE.md and the rules of RUN.md follow the tag's template: WebKit and Firefox beside Chrome Headless Shell in the stage `toolchain`, the container's name `app` on its own network, rule 4 with the exception of R146 spec, and rule 9 for a task outside an epic when there is no epic. The container also mounts the volume `novatrail-dev-token` of `api`'s development token, only to read it. The row «в реестр spec» В61 is closed: `spec` has recorded it. What the tag brings for `app` is laid out so: the path in the browser without the author and the three engines in CI — by this same task, T57; the next version of a legal text and «Who we are» — T58, before 2026-11-27; the stored works — a task of its own once `api` answers `WorkService` (Д16); the export of version 2 — E4; the proxy to `api` by its name — once `api` has the alias (Д21).** The author's decision of 2026-11-13 — T57 is the move to the latest tag of `spec`, with T56 and T51 in it —, and the answers of 2026-11-14 in its planning (RUN.md, T57, «Решено автором»).
- **What the tag brings `app`** (`git -C spec diff v0.16.3 v0.17.1`, read 2026-11-13 and 2026-11-14; sources 9.4). v0.17.1 took in v0.16.4–v0.17.0 — the tasks T141 and T144–T149 of `spec`, T115 and its inbox triage at the end of its E10:
  - the template: WebKit 2388 and Firefox 1572 from the same image of Playwright 1.65.0, a `COPY` each, and 78 packages from the snapshot `20261021T000000Z` — the libraries of the three engines and the fonts of Playwright's list `tools` without `xvfb` (T141; the 2026-11-13 amendment to R79 spec; Д217 `spec`); the browsers are named exceptions by R12 spec (its 2026-11-13 amendment); the default font becomes WenQuanYi Zen Hei instead of Liberation, in Chromium too; `--network-alias=<repository>` right after the repository's own network (T115; Д195 `spec`); rule 4 of RUN.md with the exception of R146 spec (T146); rule 9 — where the body of a task outside an epic goes when there is no current epic (the 2026-11-13 amendment to R19 spec; Д235 `spec`); a sentence in «Environment» of CLAUDE.md on the parts that `.gitignore` keeps out of git (T131 tutor);
  - the contract is only added to, and a build that does not know the additions works as before (R83 spec): `GetLegalTextsResponse.next_texts` — the next version of each text before it applies, and `in_force_since` is now the day from which a version applies, which may be later than the day it was approved (T145; the 2026-11-13 amendments to R130 and R115 spec; Д231 `spec`); `WorkService` — `PutWork`, `ListWorks`, `GetWork` and `DeleteWork`, a stored work up to 4 MiB, and the causes `CAUSE_STORAGE_OFF` and `CAUSE_WORK_TOO_LARGE` (T144; the 2026-11-13 amendments to R113 and R135 spec; Д233 `spec`);
  - the part `legal/`: version 3 of `privacy-policy`, which applies from 2026-11-27 (T148), and the passport of format 3; the types `legalv3` and `exportv2` instead of `legalv2` and `exportv1`, which `app` does not import; `types/canvas` — the comments of `SheetSubmission` and `WorkbookSubmission`: they are sent only within a stored work, while the storage of the profile's works is on;
  - Д237 `spec` — `API_APP_ORIGIN` of `api` in the local run is the origin of `app` on loopback (section 3.7);
  - the answer to В61 — the 2026-11-13 amendment to R76 spec: the site gets the pair `app` v0.1.3 with `api` v0.1.35 or newer, by a task of `infra` outside an epic, row 6.16 of [COORDINATION.md](../spec/COORDINATION.md);
  - T147 — `LICENSE` and `NOTICE` of a module are in the language of the license (`format/README.md`): `app` shows a module's license as `<pre lang="en">` since T41 (R32), which holds while the official modules are Apache-2.0 in English and the licenses of third-party modules are О-10 (the 2026-11-14 amendment to R32);
  - the part `deployment/`, the module format 0.4, `types/formatv0` and `types/admissionv0` are as at v0.16.3.
- **What changes here:**
  - `spec/` is at v0.17.1, commit 54c5f83; `gh release verify v0.17.1 -R altnovatrail/spec` gives "✓ Release v0.17.1 verified!"; `spec_tag` is v0.17.1, and `parts/deployment-1.tar.gz` with its signature comes from the release v0.17.1: `release-verify` gives "deployment-1.tar.gz: spec v0.17.1, signed by spec's release.yml";

WebKit and Firefox are `app`'s by the 2026-11-13 amendment to R79 spec, as Chrome Headless Shell is (R14);
  - CLAUDE.md, README, RUN.md and the comments of `src/canvas/jsx.ts` and `src/skills/jsx.ts` name v0.17.1; rule 4 of RUN.md has the template's four points of the exception word for word, and rule 9 the template's sentence for when there is no epic, in the words of R41: `app` has no section «Задачи вне эпика»;
  - section 3.7 says `API_APP_ORIGIN` as Д237 `spec` has it, and that the proxy goes to `http://api:8080` once `api` has its alias: T74 of `api` gives it (Д31 of `api`; Д21);
  - table 3 of RUN.md is without В61; table 2, E4, has the export of version 2; DEBT.md — Д16 waits for `WorkService` of `api`, T75 of `api`; Д17 is T58 now; the new Д21 — the proxy by name.
- **Without the new methods.** Nothing of `app` calls `WorkService` or reads `next_texts` yet, and the list of methods that `api` may not have (R40) gets nothing: T58 reads `next_texts`, and from an `api` without it shows no next version, as the line of the quota by R40.

— *Why now:* v0.17.1 is the latest release (`gh release list -R altnovatrail/spec`, 2026-11-14). It holds what T56 and T51 waited for — WebKit and Firefox in the template, rule 4 by R146 spec —, and `api` on `main` has the stub and the token of R66 and R67 api. Version 3 of `privacy-policy` applies from 2026-11-27, and `app` must show it before that: the contract of T145 is in this tag.

— *Rejected:*
- the intermediate v0.17.0: T145 and T148 are in both, but v0.17.1 is the latest and came out four hours later;
- the next version of a text in T57 — one more check in a task the author keeps whole; the author's answer of 2026-11-14 — T58 right after it;
- the stored works now — `api` does not answer `WorkService` on `main`, so there would be nothing to check them against;
- the proxy by name `http://api:8080` now — `api`'s container has no alias until its own move, T74 of `api`.

— *Consequence:* RUN.md — T57, T58, table 1, «Почему такой порядок», rules 4 and 9 and tables 2 and 3; the 2026-11-14 amendments to R13, R14, R15, R19, R32, R38, R41 and R42; DEBT.md — Д16, Д17 and Д21; [plan/E3.md](plan/E3.md) — the closed row В61; CLAUDE.md; README.md; `justfile`; `parts/`; `NOTICE`; `package.json`; `.devcontainer/`; [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.7.

## The path in a browser without the author

**R44. The local build signs in by `api`'s development token, and the path of the first slice runs in a browser with no one at the keyboard: `just walk`, by hand and not in CI, on Chromium, WebKit and Firefox against `api`'s `just dev-app`. Its AI calls go to the stub of `tutor`, and no real one is made. The proxy of the development server goes to `api` by its name.** The plan of T57, part B, which the author approved with it (RUN.md, T57; Д227 `spec`, the answer to В52).
- **The sign-in of the local build.** «Sign in with Google» on the development server first asks it for `/__novatrail/custom-token` and, with a token there, signs in by `signInWithCustomToken`, with no window; with none — `just dev-app` is not running —, by the window as before. The branch stands behind `import.meta.env.DEV`, which Vite replaces with `false` in the build, so `src/signin/local.ts` is not in the build at all; the plugin `scripts/local-token.ts` is `apply: "serve"` and serves the file of the volume `novatrail-dev-token` (R43; R67 api), read again at each request, since `api` writes a new token every half hour, or 404; `vite preview` has no such path, and the test of the built page holds that no file of `build/` names it. The path is a constant of `src/signin/token-path.ts`, shared by the app and the plugin.
- **The proxy** goes to `http://api:8080` by default: `api`'s container has the name `api` since T74 of `api` (Д31 of `api`), and `NOVATRAIL_API` names another address. Д21 is closed. Plain HTTP, which SonarCloud flags (S5332) and the line marks `NOSONAR` with the reason: `api`'s development server has no TLS, the requests stay on the Docker network of the machine, and a release's build has no proxy.
- **The walk** — `walk/path.test.ts` with its own `walk/vitest.config.ts`, outside `src/` and `scripts/`, so that `ci-test` and the coverage do not take it. It starts the development server by Vite's API with `vite.config.ts` and walks, in each engine `just walk` names, in a new context: the sign-in and My subjects with the line of the tutor's time; a lesson; a task of the skill map — in handwriting through the stub's reading of the crop's shape, by tap otherwise —; the section for adults; the sign-out; «Erase everything on this device». The steps are in architecture 3.7, "Tests". A request to a host the sign-in does not name, or an error in the console, fails it.
- **The stub and the quota.** The stub answers by the crop's shape — wider than tall correct, taller than wide not, square illegible (R66 api) —, and the crop has a side of 200 px at least: the walk writes 25-unit upright dashes, one for a square crop, eight in a column for a tall one and eight in a row for a wide one. Each call spends the quota of the profile's day as the real one would (R55 api): so the full sequence runs once per engine, on the skill map's task, and a lesson's tasks are ended by their solution. With the quota spent, the walk stops at its first step and says so; restarting `just dev-app` with another `API_DEV_APP_UID` gives a new account.
- **What it found** (measurements 3): two sentences of a status ran together with no full stop — fixed here, on the screen of what's next and the skill map; in WebKit, Google's page in the hidden iframe asks `www.googleapis.com`, which architecture 3.12 spec does not name — row В62 «в реестр spec», and section 3.7 names the host; in WebKit, once of three runs, the heartbeats' database was still there 15 s after the tab was closed (Д13).
- **DEBT.md:** Д14 is left with the task by tap, which the engine gave in no run; Д20 is closed — the line of the tutor's time and the lesson's name in the progress were seen against `api` with its `GetQuota`; Д13 has the heartbeats in WebKit.

— *Why:* the executor has no Google sign-in (T36, T42), so the path of the first slice against a real `api` waited for the author; `api` gives its development token for just this (R67 api), and Д227 `spec` answers В52 with the path in a browser by Playwright. The same button in the same app keeps what is walked what the learner has.

— *Rejected:*
- a button or a global hook of its own for the walk — lines and a path no learner has;
- the Firebase Auth emulator — `just dev-app` signs in on the real dev project (R67 api);
- the walk in CI — it needs `api` with its stub, the dev project and the token, which CI has none of;
- the walk on `vite preview` — the build has no path of the token, by design;
- `NOVATRAIL_API` as a requirement — `api`'s name makes it the exception;
- the full stub sequence on every task — a day's quota would not hold three engines.

— *Consequence:* `src/signin/` — `local.ts`, `token-path.ts` and the branch of `firebase.ts`; `scripts/local-token.ts`; `vite.config.ts`; `walk/`; `justfile` — `walk`; `src/next/NextStep.tsx` and `src/skills/SkillMapScreen.tsx`; `tsconfig.node.json`, `biome.json`, [.claude/rules/code.md](../.claude/rules/code.md); README.md; [architecture.md](architecture.md), section 3.7; [measurements.md](measurements.md), section 3; DEBT.md — Д13, Д14, Д20 and Д21; RUN.md, table 3 — В62; the 2026-11-14 amendments to R03 and R19.
